- The folder imports nothing from `components/shell/`, `components/phone/`, `components/device/` or 3D code, and is in the field lint block.
- EZD-126 extends the toast `tone`.
- The test page is `/dev/states` (`?lang=es` shows Spanish; not found in production).

### Disclaimer (EZD-136)

EZD-136 (vault/plans/EZD-136.md Steps 1-19); 04 App shell; Q-0208; Q-0229; Q-0272 (a); Q-0273 (b); Q-0305 (c); AC-EZD-136-2.

- **Component.** `Disclaimer` (`app/src/components/disclaimer/Disclaimer.tsx`, client, `DisclaimerPlacement` `'bar' | 'page'`) renders one `<p data-testid="disclaimer" data-placement="bar|page">` with `t('common.disclaimer')`; never a `<footer>` or `data-status`. It imports only `useT` and its CSS module. The folder is in the field lint block (`common.`, `field.`, `error.`).
- **Where.** `bar`: the last child of the editor `StatusBar` and of the Settings status bar (`SettingsShell`), right of every readout. `page`: the last element of the page: `/` (Projects page, after `main`), `/sign-in` and `/auth/confirm` (bottom of the canvas, the card centred above it), the phone screens (last child of `PhoneFrame`'s main: `EditorPhoneNotice`, the field and client entry pages) and the not-found page (`app/src/app/home.module.css`, which now serves `not-found.tsx` only). Each page's own `MessagesProvider` decides the language.
- **Text.** `common.disclaimer` (en Q-0208; es W1) and `error.page_not_found` (en "Page not found.", Q-0273 (b); es "Página no encontrada."), both in `en` and `es`.
- **Narrow status bar** (Q-0272 (a)): `.bar` is `flex: 0 1000 auto` with an ellipsis and `StatusBar.module.css` `.start` is `flex: 1 1 auto`, so the readouts keep their width and the line gives way first; the full text stays in the DOM and in `title` (on `bar` only).
- **Every page.** Every `page.tsx` and `not-found.tsx` under `app/src/app/` outside `app/src/app/dev/` has one row in `DISCLAIMER_PAGES` (`app/test-harness/e2e/disclaimer-pages.ts`, U6). A later ticket that adds such a file renders `Disclaimer` and adds its row.
- **Not-found page.** `app/src/app/not-found.tsx` (status 404): "Page not found." and the line, no link or control. Language through `getNotFoundLanguage()` (`app/src/server/messages/not-found-language.ts`, Q-0305 (c)): the signed-in person's `app_user.language` (`getRequestLanguage('field')`), else the device's sign-in cookie (`getSignInLanguage()`); a failed read other than "not signed in" is reported (`not_found.language`) and falls back to the cookie. The request gate and `PUBLIC_PATHS` are unchanged, so signed out it is reached only under a public entry (`/dev/...`).
- **Never in outputs** (AC-EZD-136-2): no file under `app/src/server/jobs/`, `app/src/lazy/`, `app/src/workers/`, `packages/geometry/src/` or `worker/src/` names `common.disclaimer`, `components/disclaimer` or the text (U7), so sheets and PDFs never carry it. No `@media print` rule.

### Entry points and lazy modules (EZD-020)

EZD-020 (vault/plans/EZD-020.md Steps 1-4); 11 Performance (Budgets Download size, rules 3 and 7); 04 ("the only separate pages are Projects, Settings, the phone field pack and the client link").

- **Entry points are route groups**, each with its own `layout.tsx` (a server component rendering `<div data-entry="<name>" style={{ display: 'contents' }}>`); code imported only by one group's layout and pages reaches only that group's pages.

| Group | `data-entry` | Entry (11 Budgets) | Placeholder (404 in production) | Real pages from |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `app/src/app/(office)/` | `office` | Projects and Settings, 200 KB | `/dev/entry/office` | EZD-117a (`/`), EZD-103 (`/settings/...`) |
| `app/src/app/(editor)/` | `editor` | Editor before its lazy parts, 500 KB | `/dev/entry/editor` | EZD-120c (`/projects/[id]`) |
| `app/src/app/(field)/` | `field` | Field pack, 200 KB | `/dev/entry/field` | EZD-1404 |
| `app/src/app/(client)/` | `client` | Client link, 200 KB | `/dev/entry/client` | EZD-1202 |

- The placeholders carry what every page of the entry will carry (`MessagesProvider`, `StateHost`, and `EmptyEditor` or `PhoneFrame`), so the perf check measures something now; each owner ticket adds its real pages in the group and repoints the measurements (`### Entry contents and bundle report (EZD-020)`).
- **One root layout.** `app/src/app/layout.tsx` stays the only root layout and carries only what every entry needs; anything else goes in a group layout.
- `/` is Projects (`app/src/app/(office)/page.tsx`, EZD-117a).
- The client link adds its path to `isPublicPath` (EZD-007 Step 4; EZD-1202).
- **`app/src/editor/`** is reserved for editor-only code that is not a shell component (store, canvas, tools; EZD-120b, EZD-207). `three` and `@react-three/*` may be imported statically there (the plan canvas, 02 Stack 2D + 3D), never in Projects, Settings, the field pack, the client link or shared code.
- **Lazy modules.** A lazy module is a folder `app/src/lazy/<name>/` with an `index.ts` or `index.tsx`; only files inside it import its heavy library statically. Code outside the folder reaches it only by `import()`: plain modules through `lazyModule(() => import('.../lazy/<name>'))` (`app/src/lib/lazy/lazy-module.ts`: `load()`, `preload()`, `peek()`; one promise in flight, a rejected load is tried again on the next `load()`); React components through `next/dynamic(() => import('.../lazy/<name>'), { ssr: false })`, rendered only when the user opens the feature (a dynamic component mounted at load downloads at load), or through `lazyModule` when the caller shows its own load error (EZD-108 `my-profile/`). `import type` from a lazy module is allowed (types are erased).
- Lazy-only libraries: the GLB and USDZ exporters (`three/examples/jsm/exporters/`, `three/addons/exporters/`), `three-bvh-csg`, `three-gpu-pathtracer`, `pdfjs-dist`, `dxf-parser` and the DXF writer. Allowed statically only in `app/src/lazy/`, Web Workers (`app/src/workers/`) and, for pdf.js and the DXF libraries, job handlers (`app/src/server/jobs/`).
- `<Link>`s from the office, field and client entries into the editor use `prefetch={false}`; the contents check reports editor code that a prefetch pulls in.
- Reserved lazy-module names (each folder is created by its owner ticket):

| Folder | Holds | Owner |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `scene-3d/` | perspective 3D scene and meshes | EZD-1001a; EZD-1013 (phone viewer) reuses it or adds `phone-3d/` |
| `pathtracer/` | three-gpu-pathtracer | EZD-1105 |
| `pdf/` | pdf.js underlay | EZD-304 |
| `dxf-writer/` | DXF writer (browser use only, if any) | EZD-913 |
| `exporters/` | GLB and USDZ (or a worker in `app/src/workers/`) | EZD-1014 |
| `sheets/` | sheet renderer | EZD-702b |
| `detail-editor/` | Detail editor | EZD-801 |
| `catalog-drawer/` | catalog drawer | EZD-1601 |
| `reference/` | reference drawing in Plan | EZD-901b |
| `my-profile/` | My profile dialog | EZD-108 |
| `notification-panel/` | the bell's notification list | EZD-332 |
| `template-picker/` | Template picker in New project step 2 | EZD-221b |

- `app/src/lazy/dev-sample/` is the dev-only proof module, loaded by `/dev/lazy` on first press.
- **One list.** `eslint-rules/boundaries.mjs` (`LAZY_ROOT`, `LAZY_PLACES`, `EDITOR_CODE`, `EDITOR_CODE_IMPORTERS`, `PATH_ALIASES`, `HEAVY_PACKAGES`, `ENTRY_RULES`, `WATCH_PACKAGES`) is the single list read by the lint rule `ezdraft/import-boundaries` and by the contents check; EZD-029's replace path edits the DXF names there, and a test fails when that list and the Named tools `DXF` row drift apart.
- The lint rule (`eslint-rules/import-boundaries.mjs`, block in `eslint.config.mjs` for `app/src/**` and `packages/*/src/**`, test files ignored) reports `heavyPackage`, `lazyStatic` (a static import into `app/src/lazy/<name>/` from outside it) and `editorOnly` (editor code imported outside `EDITOR_CODE_IMPORTERS`). It resolves relative paths and the tsconfig path aliases in `PATH_ALIASES` (`@/` = `app/src/`, for files under `app/`; EZD-167); a ticket that adds a `paths` or `baseUrl` entry to a tsconfig adds it there in the same commit, or `import-boundaries-lint.test.mjs` (A6) fails. Transitive imports through shared code are caught by the contents check, not the lint.

### PWA (EZD-013)

EZD-013 (vault/plans/EZD-013.md Steps 1-12); 02 Platform; R24 #1; Q-0051 (a); Q-0053; Q-0088.

- Files: `app/src/lib/pwa/app-meta.ts` (`APP_NAME` `EZ Draft`, `THEME_COLOR` read from the `--ez-canvas` entry of `TOKENS`), `app/src/app/manifest.ts` (served at `/manifest.webmanifest`), `app/src/lib/pwa/register-sw.ts` (`registerServiceWorker`), `app/src/components/pwa/ServiceWorkerRegistration.tsx` (mounted last in `<body>` of the root layout), `app/public/sw.js`, `app/next.config.ts` (the `/sw.js` header), `app/src/app/layout.tsx` (`appleWebApp`, `viewport`), `app/src/pwa/app-icon.html` and `app/scripts/app-icons.mjs`.
- Manifest values: name and short name `EZ Draft` (R24 #1), `display` `standalone`, `start_url` and `scope` `/`, `id` `/`, `lang` `en`, `background_color` and `theme_color` `#f6f7f9` (`--ez-canvas`, 04 Frame and visual system), three icons (192 `any`, 512 `any`, 512 `maskable`). No description and no orientation.
- `/sw.js` with scope `/` is fixed: EZD-134 builds the Workbox worker at the same URL or unregisters this one.
- No fetch handler and no cache until EZD-134; the worker only calls `skipWaiting` and `clients.claim`.
- `registerServiceWorker` registers after the window `load` event, only in a secure context with service worker support, and reports a failed registration with `captureBrowserError(error, 'pwa.register')`, loaded with `import()`, and never throws.
- Re-render the icons: edit `app/src/pwa/app-icon.html`, run `pnpm --filter @ezdraft/app app-icons:render`, commit the five PNGs.
- Public static files keep a file extension, so the request gate (`app/src/proxy.ts` matcher) never sees them.

### Settings shell (EZD-103a)

EZD-103a (vault/plans/EZD-103a.md Steps 4-12); S15 Layout, Entry points; Q-0210; Q-0214; Q-0226; Q-0232; Q-0234.

- **Files and exports.** `app/src/lib/settings/sections.ts` (`SettingsSectionId`, `SettingsSection`, `SETTINGS_SECTIONS` in nav order, `settingsHref`, `sectionBySlug`, `sectionForPath`, `allowedSections`); `app/src/lib/users/initials.ts` (`initialsFor`); `app/src/components/settings/SettingsShell.tsx` (`SettingsShell`, `SettingsShellProps`, `SettingsMain`, `SettingsLoadError`) with `SettingsShell.module.css`; `app/src/server/settings/shell-data.ts` (`SettingsShellData`, `loadSettingsShellData`, `settingsPermissionContext`); `app/src/server/settings/settings-root.tsx` (`SettingsRoot`); `app/src/app/(office)/settings/layout.tsx`, `page.tsx` and `[section]/page.tsx`.
- **Routes.** `/settings` sends a 307 to the first section the user may open, in nav order, and shows `NoAccess` in place when there is none; `/settings/<slug>` serves the ten sections (an unknown slug is not found). `[section]` serves a section's page (its title only) until a page ticket adds `app/src/app/(office)/settings/<slug>/page.tsx`, which takes precedence.
- **Nav filtering on the server.** `SettingsRoot` passes `allowed` = `allowedSections((p) => can(ctx, p))`; a section without its `settings_*` switch is absent, never disabled; no `PermissionsProvider` is mounted.
- **Page-ticket contract.** A Settings page renders inside `SettingsMain`, reads the context through `settingsPermissionContext()` (one load per request, shared with the layout; `'failed'` renders `null`, the layout shows the error), checks `can(ctx, <its settings_* switch>)` itself and renders `NoAccess` inside `SettingsMain` when false (HTTP 200, URL unchanged).
- **Load failure.** When the permission context or the shell data cannot be read, the layout renders `SettingsLoadError` (`error.db_unavailable` with Retry, `router.refresh()`) instead of the page and reports `settings_shell_load_failed` with location `settings.shell`.
- **Shell parts never import `components/shell/`** (editor code; the office entry forbids it): the card, header and status bar CSS are rebuilt from tokens.

### Unsaved edits and drafts (EZD-103b)

EZD-103b (vault/plans/EZD-103b.md Steps 3-13); S15 Layout, Entry points, Validation; Q-0228; Q-0265 (b); Q-0302.

- **Files and exports.** `app/src/components/states/confirm-controller.ts` (`ConfirmChoice` adds `'discard'`; `discardLabel?` on `ConfirmOptions` and `ConfirmRequest`, absent when not given) and `ConfirmDialog.tsx` (with `discardLabel` the footer is Cancel, `button[data-confirm-discard]`, then the action); `app/src/lib/settings/draft.ts` (`DraftRecord`, `DraftStore`, `DraftPage`, `draftKey`, `restoreDraft`, `syncDraft`, `toDraftRecord`; no React, no `idb`); `app/src/components/settings/draft-store.ts` (`SETTINGS_DRAFT_DB`, `SETTINGS_DRAFT_STORE`, `createIdbDraftStore`); `app/src/components/settings/unsaved-changes.tsx` (`UnsavedEntry`, `LeaveResult`, `confirmLeave`, `handleHistoryBack`, `isOriginEntry`, `UnsavedChangesProvider`, `useUnsavedChanges`, `useSettingsDraft`, `useLeave`, `GuardedLink`, `GuardedLinkProps`); `SettingsShell` takes `draftScope` and wraps its header, body and status bar in `UnsavedChangesProvider`, with the Projects link and every nav item a `GuardedLink`; `SettingsRoot` passes `draftScope` = the membership id, else the app user id; the test page `/dev/settings` (`?fail=1` makes Save fail; not found in production) in `app/src/app/(office)/dev/settings/` (`page.tsx`, `UnsavedSample.tsx`).
- **Page contract.** Every Settings page calls `useUnsavedChanges(dirty, save)` and `useSettingsDraft(<section id>, dirty ? form : null, restore)`; `save` is stable (`useCallback`) and resolves `true` when saved, `false` after showing its own error; the draft value is structured-cloneable, keeps its identity while unchanged and is `null` when the page is clean. One page registers at a time.
- **Unsaved prompt.** Leaving a dirty page through a `GuardedLink` or `useLeave()` asks `office.settings.unsaved_title` ("Save your changes?") with Cancel, Discard, Save (Q-0228): Save runs the page's `save` (a throw counts as failed) and leaves only when it succeeds, clearing the draft; Discard clears the draft and leaves; Cancel stays. A clean page leaves with no prompt.
- **Browser Back and Forward, leaving the tab.** On the first edit the current entry is marked `{ ezdOrigin: <uuid> }` with `history.replaceState` and one extra entry is pushed at the same URL (Q-0265 (b)); Back onto the marked entry asks the same prompt (Cancel or a failed Save pushes the extra entry again; a clean armed page leaves in one press); a jump of two or more entries goes straight to the destination, and the draft keeps the edits. While armed there is no forward entry, so Forward does nothing. A guarded leave while armed uses `router.replace`, so the extra entry is replaced and Back lands on the Settings page once. The extra entry is never removed. While dirty one `beforeunload` listener warns on closing the tab.
- **Draft store and lifetime.** IndexedDB database `ezdraft-settings-drafts` v1, store `drafts`, key `<draftScope>:<page>`, value `{ value }` (Q-0302); `idb` is imported only in `draft-store.ts` (which opens and closes the database on every call) and `src/lib/outbox/outbox-idb.ts` (Q-0596). A failure is reported with `captureBrowserError(error, 'settings.draft')` (loaded with `import()`) and the call resolves (`read` with `null`), so the page keeps working and the prompt and tab warning still protect the edits. A draft is written on each change while dirty, cleared when the page turns clean or by the prompt's Save or Discard, never by time; it stays after sign-out until EZD-134 decides. On return the draft is restored with one `office.settings.draft_restored` toast, unless the person typed before the read finished.
- **Refs.** Every `useRef` variable and every ref field of the context ends in `Ref`; refs are read and written only in effects, callbacks and event handlers, never during render (the `react-hooks/refs` and `react-hooks/immutability` lint); the latest value and restore callback are copied into refs by an effect with no dependency array, declared first.

### Patch store and outbox (EZD-120b)

EZD-120b (vault/plans/EZD-120b.md Build steps 1-10, section 3); 03a; 03b rules 1, 4, 6, 9; Q-0501; Q-0503; Q-0507.

- **Model store** (`app/src/editor/model/`). `patches.ts`: `Change` `{ id, value }` (a null value is a delete), `Objects` (a read-only map of id to `ObjectValue`), `Patch` `{ id, before, after }` (a null `before` is an add, a null `after` a remove), `Edit` `{ patches }`, `makeEdit(objects, changes)` (an id twice throws `duplicate_object_id`) and `applyEditTo(objects, edit, 'forward' | 'back')`, which returns a new map in which every value it did not touch keeps its identity. `model-store.ts`: `createModelStore({ objects, onCommit? })`, a `zustand/vanilla` store of `{ objects, past, future, lastChangedIds }` with `applyEdit(changes)`, `undo()` and `redo()`; each returns the changed ids in patch order, sets `lastChangedIds` and calls `onCommit` once with those changes; an empty edit returns `[]`, calls nothing and is not recorded; `applyEdit` clears `future`; undo and redo move the same `Edit` between `past` and `future`, with no depth limit.
- **Load.** `GET /api/projects/[id]/model` (`force-dynamic`, `no-store`): 401, 403 without View projects, 404 `project_not_found` (one body for an unknown, malformed, other-company or out-of-scope project), 500 `db_unavailable` (reported), else 200 `ModelLoad` (`app/src/lib/outbox/model-api.ts`: the project's id, name and client name, the viewer with `canEdit` = `can(ctx, 'edit_model', { projectId })`, the edit lock's `lockEpoch` (0 without a lock row), the live objects by id, and the objects that fail to parse in `invalid`). `loadModel(client, projectId, ctx)` (`app/src/server/model/model.ts`) reads through the session client (RLS), pages the objects 1,000 at a time by `object_id` and reads each with `parseStoredObject`. `fetchModel(projectId)` gives `{ ok: true, model }` or `{ ok: false, status, code }` (a thrown fetch is status 0, `network`); `loadWorkingCopy(load, entries)` lays that project's outbox entries over the loaded objects.
- **Device outbox** (`app/src/lib/outbox/outbox-idb.ts`, the only importer of `idb` besides `draft-store.ts`). IndexedDB database `ezdraft-device` v1, stores `outbox` and `working_copy`, both keyed by app user, company, project and object id; a storage built for one user and company reads only its own key range (Q-0503). One connection, opened on first use and closed by `close()` or when a newer version asks. `put` writes a new entry with `baseSeq` from the `working_copy` mirror (or 0), `rev` 1 and `queuedAt`, or gives an existing entry the new value and `rev + 1`. `commit` (after a save) deletes an entry whose `rev` did not change, else re-bases it on the saved `updatedSeq`, and writes the mirror (a saved delete removes it); `drop` deletes an unchanged entry; `replaceMirror` writes the loaded objects' sequence numbers.
- **Sync** (`app/src/lib/outbox/outbox-sync.ts`, `createOutboxSync`, a `zustand/vanilla` store of `SyncState`). `enqueue` writes the outbox and restarts one 2,000 ms timer; the drain runs under the tab lock `ezdraft-outbox:<appUserId>:<tenantId>` (`navigator.locks` with `ifAvailable`; a busy lock tries again in 2,000 ms) and sends one `POST /api/projects/<id>/objects` at a time, at most 100 changes of the oldest project, until the outbox is empty, a save is refused or fails, or the timer is pending. A delete of an object never saved is dropped, not sent. Refused: a refusal for good moves those changes to the conflict list (`### Sync conflicts on the device (EZD-299b)`); any other refusal keeps the entries, status `error` with the code, no drain until `retry()`. Failed (any other answer or a thrown fetch): entries kept, tried again after 2, 4, 8, 16, then every 30 seconds, `error` from the third failure in a row. Offline: status `offline`, no drain until the browser is back online. `saved` with `lastSavedAt` when a drain empties the outbox; a storage failure is reported with `captureBrowserError(error, 'outbox.storage')` and shows `error` with `storage_failed`. A save never makes a checkpoint (03a).

### Sync conflicts on the device (EZD-299b)

EZD-299b (vault/plans/EZD-299b.md Build steps 1-9, section 3); 03b rule 9; Q-0502; Q-0511; Q-0531; Q-0540; Q-0631 (b).

- **Key rule.** The device database stays `ezdraft-device` version 1: a new kind of device record goes in an existing store under its own first key part, never in a new store or a new version (an older open tab would fail to reopen and lose the edits it holds). Conflict rows (`DeviceConflict`, `app/src/lib/outbox/outbox.ts`) live in the `outbox` store under `[CONFLICT_KEY_PART ('conflict'), appUserId, tenantId, projectId, objectId]`, read with `conflictRange(appUserId, tenantId)` (`app/src/lib/outbox/device-db.ts`), outside `actorRange`, so `list()`, `countQueued` and an older tab never see them. `OutboxStorage` adds `moveToConflicts` (an entry still at the sent `rev` leaves the outbox for a row `listed`, server `pending`, or `device` for `not_open`; an existing row's server id is kept), `listConflicts`, `setConflictServer` (a missing row is returned as an orphan; a newer refusal takes only the server id), `deleteConflicts` (given a `refusedAt`, it deletes only a stored row with that `refusedAt` and returns the keys it kept), `requeue` (the row becomes `requeued`, its change goes back in the outbox at the given base; a stored row with another `refusedAt`, a newer refusal, is left alone and `{ kept: true }` returned) and `setMirror`.
- **Refusals** (`outbox-sync.ts`). `classifySave` adds `conflicts` for 409 `save_conflict` and `invalid` for 400 `invalid_object`. A save conflict moves the listed changes (reason `conflict`, with the server seq); a schema-check refusal moves the listed changes (reason `invalid`) and is reported as `outbox.invalid_object`; 403 `permission_denied` moves every change of that project (`not_editable`) and 404 `project_not_found` every change of that project as `not_open`, one reason for a deleted project and one no longer open to the person (Q-0631 (b)), after dropping its unsent deletes. The drain then goes on with `conflictRev` + 1. Every other refusal, and one that names no queued change, blocks as before.
- **Record.** At the end of every lock run, online, the rows `listed` and `pending` are sent with `POST /api/projects/<id>/sync-conflicts`, grouped by project, 1,000 per call; a row whose `data` JSON is over 65,536 bytes stays on the device. 201 records the server ids; 404 keeps the rows on the device as `not_open`; 400 keeps them on the device and is reported as `outbox.record`; any other answer leaves them pending for the next drain (a failed call stops the pass). A row removed during the call has its new server copy deleted.
- **Resolves.** `keepMine(row, baseSeq)` requeues the row on the write chain and drains. At send time a sent change with a `requeued` row that has a server id names that id in the save's `resolves`; `commit` deletes the `requeued` rows at the saved keys in the same transaction.
- **List** (`app/src/lib/conflicts/conflict-list.ts`, `createConflictList`, a `zustand/vanilla` store of `{ rows, server }`; calls in `conflict-api.ts`). `refresh()` merges the device rows with `GET /api/sync-conflicts` (`mergeConflicts`: one row per key, a requeued row with a queued change hidden, a recorded row missing from the server's list deleted as resolved elsewhere); a result is applied only when no newer refresh started and nothing changed the list meanwhile, else it is dropped or run again. Each action first re-reads the device row: a newer refusal at the key since the list was read refreshes the list and fails, changing nothing; device rows are deleted only at the `refusedAt` the list showed, and the server id comes from the device row when the list's is not yet set. Keep mine (reason `conflict` only, with a sync) requeues the change at the saved seq; Keep theirs takes the saved value (or the server model's), deletes the server copy, the row and sets the mirror; Remove deletes the server copy and the row. The open editor takes the chosen value with `ModelStore.applyRemote` (no undo step, nothing queued). `openEditorSession` builds the list as `session.conflicts`, refreshes it after the mirror and disposes it first.

### Editor route (EZD-120c)

EZD-120c (vault/plans/EZD-120c.md Build steps 1-10, section 3); 03a; S03 Permissions, Data shown, States; Q-0500; Q-0214; Q-0501; Q-0505; Q-0506.

- **Route.** `/projects/[id]` is `app/src/app/(editor)/projects/[id]/page.tsx`: `connection()`, `requireSession()` (signed out goes to `/sign-in`), then `MessagesProvider` (office, en) › `StateHost` › `ProjectEditor` (`app/src/editor/ProjectEditor.tsx`, props `{ projectId, devHooks }`, `devHooks` = `VERCEL_ENV` is not `production`). Project cards (`<Link prefetch={false}>`) and New project's create open it.
- **Open.** `openEditorSession(projectId, deps?)` (`app/src/editor/editor-session.ts`) answers `OpenResult`: from `fetchModel`, 403 `no-access`, 404 `not-found`, anything else `failed`; `ready` holds an `EditorSession` `{ model, store, sync, dispose() }`. Ready: the storage for the viewer's ids, the device outbox laid over the load (`loadWorkingCopy`; a list that throws counts as empty), `createModelStore`, `replaceMirror` with the loaded objects; an editor (`canEdit`) gets `createOutboxSync`, each commit is enqueued with the load's `lockEpoch`, and a non-empty outbox is flushed at once; view-only gets no sync and saves nothing. A device storage failure is reported with `captureBrowserError(error, 'editor.device_copy')`. `dispose()` disposes the sync and closes the storage; the editor calls it on unmount, and on a session that arrives after unmount.
- **Screen.** Every state: `DeviceLayout` (phone: `EditorPhoneNotice`) with `EditorFrame` (no rail, panels or status items yet) and `StatusBar`. Header: `HeaderCard` with the wordmark until ready, then `EditorHeader` (project name; subtitle client, "Working copy" and, for an editor, `SaveStateText`; no view tabs). Canvas: `editor-state` (`data-editor-state` `loading`, `failed`, `no-access`, `not-found`, `ready`) shows a skeleton, `InlineError` `office.editor.load_failed` with Retry, `NoAccess`, `editor-not-found` (`error.page_not_found`), or, ready for an editor, `OfflineBanner` (`office.editor.offline`) and `SaveFailedBanner` (`save-failed-banner`, status `error`, Retry = `sync.retry()`).
- **Save word** (`app/src/editor/save-state.ts`, `SaveStatus.tsx`). `saveWord(state, now, timeZone?)` gives the `office.editor.*` key: idle and saved without a time `saved`, pending and saving `saving`, offline `saved_on_device`, error `not_saved`, saved under a minute `saved_just_now`, under an hour `saved_min_ago` (`minutes`), else `saved_at` (`formatClock`, `en-US` h:mm AM/PM). `SaveStateText` (`save-state`, `data-save-status`) re-renders once when the word next changes (`nextSaveWordChangeMs`, one `setTimeout`), never on a tick.
- **Test hook.** Ready, `canEdit` and `devHooks`: `window.__ezdraftEditor` (`EditorTestHook`: `objects()` lists the store's objects as `{ id, ...value }`, `edit(changes)` is `store.applyEdit`), removed on unmount. Never in production, never for view-only.
- Strings are `office.editor.*` keys, `en` only.

### Conflict list screens (EZD-299)

EZD-299 (vault/plans/EZD-299.md Build steps 1-14, section 3); board S03b; Q-0502; Q-0508; Q-0511; Q-0540; Q-0628 (b); Q-0631 (b); Q-0632 (a).

- **Rows** (`app/src/lib/conflicts/conflict-view.ts`, pure, never throws). `conflictRowView` reads mine and saved raw: label (`objectLabel`), noun and icon by type, the changed fields (wall length, thickness, height, or position when the length is the same; opening width, height, sill height, position; else Details; lengths and points compared at 1/64"), the Yours and Saved chips (`formatLength` at 1/2"; Saved only for reason `conflict`), and why plus hint. `groupConflicts` groups rows as this project, other projects and projects no longer open (reason `not_open`, never a project name), pages them 50 at a time (`CONFLICT_PAGE_SIZE`; Show more adds 50) and counts each group in full; `countForProject` counts this project's rows.
- **Outdated rows.** While the sync's problem is `app_outdated`, `useConflictInputs` (`app/src/editor/conflicts/use-conflicts.ts`) reads `EditorSession.queued()` (the device outbox, every project) when the problem starts and each time the list opens; `outdatedInputs` makes one row per entry, newest first, key `outdated:<projectId>/<id>`, action Reload only. The save-failed banner is hidden while such rows exist.
- **Banner** (`ConflictBanner.tsx`, `conflict-banner`): under the header card when this project has rows; "Your other edits are saved." unless the sync is in error or offline; Review changes opens the list.
- **List** (`ConflictPanel.tsx`: `ConflictPanelView` pure, `ConflictPanel` the container; `conflict-panel`, `section#changes-not-saved`, `role="dialog"`, not modal): the right 720 px of the canvas region, last child of the canvas. Opening it refreshes the conflict list. Keep mine, Keep theirs and Remove call `session.conflicts`; a row is disabled while its action runs; not allowed and failed show a toast, not found refreshes. Reload reloads the page. The saved-by line (conflict rows with a saved time) uses the bell's relative time.
- **Show.** A row of the open project whose object is in the open model, while the plan canvas is up: closes the list, then `plan.handle.showObject(id)`.
- **Link.** `/projects/<id>?conflicts=open` (exactly `open`) opens the editor with the list open (`ProjectEditorProps.conflictsOpen`); the notification link uses it.
- **Bell.** A `sync_conflict` item's icon box is amber (`.iconBoxWarn`).
- **Save bench.** `/dev/saves/<id>` has Make a conflict (`bench-conflict`): `createConflictFault` sends "their" version of the next objects save first (each wall's height + 1, same headers), so the edit is refused; `waitForConflict` waits for its row; `bench-result` `data-target` names the wall.
- Strings are `office.conflicts.*` keys, `en` only.

### Plan canvas (EZD-207a)

EZD-207a (vault/plans/EZD-207a.md Build steps 1-17, section 3); S03 Layout, Canvas interactions; 02 Stack 2D + 3D; 11 rule 4; R-054; Q-0568; Q-0214.

- **Files** in `app/src/editor/plan/`: `color.ts` (token colours as sRGB 0..1), `view.ts` (the view maths, no DOM), `batches.ts` (open floor's drawing list into batches), `scene.ts` (batch objects and the grid shader), `input.ts` (pointer, wheel and key input), `controller.ts` (`createPlanController`, the handle; no React), `render-root.ts` (the React Three Fiber root), `PlanCanvas.tsx` (`PlanCanvas`, `PlanInputSurface`); EZD-207b adds `glyph-atlas.ts` (atlas cells and text layout), `glyph-atlas-canvas.ts` (draws the atlas), `hover.ts` (wall hit test, hover index, highlight shape), `ZoomStack.tsx` (the zoom stack).
- **Handle** (`PlanCanvasHandle`): `getCursor`, `getPixelsPerInch`, `getView`, `setView`, `fit`, `modelToScreen`, `screenToModel`, `onCameraChange`, `setPointerReceiver`, and from EZD-207b `zoomIn`, `zoomOut`, `showObject`, `setHoverMode`, `getHoveredWallId`, `wallAt`. Screen = region px (CSS px from the top-left of `main[data-shell-region="canvas"]`); read and called synchronously, never through React. Pans go to the view; other left input goes to the pointer receiver. EZD-208a adds `setHoverPicker`, `setBodyLookup`, `setSelectedIds`, `getHoveredId`. EZD-219 adds `getScale`, `setScale`, `getZoomPercent`, `setZoomPercent`, `onCursorChange` (`createPlanController({ store, scale? })`; EZD-235 adds `derived?` and `derivedStats()`), and from EZD-211b `onSpaceTap`.
- **View.** Model inches, +x east, +y north. 100% = 96 × scale ÷ 12 CSS px per inch for the plan scale (`zoom100Ppi`, EZD-219; 4 px per inch at the default 1/2" = 1'-0"), limits 5% and 1600% (`MIN_ZOOM_PERCENT`, `MAX_ZOOM_PERCENT`); a scale change keeps the zoom percent and moves no object. The first view fits the drawing once the region has a size; edits and resizes never refit. A pan or zoom changes only the camera and the grid uniforms, rebuilds no buffer and makes no React commit; frames are drawn on demand (`frameloop: 'demand'`).
- **Grid.** Drawn by a shader on the one `<canvas>` (`--ez-canvas-grid` on `--ez-canvas`), steps 1", 1', 10', 100' (the first at least 16 px apart). The canvas fills the frame's `backdrop`, behind header, cards and status bar.
- **Batches.** One buffer per layer and kind (segments also per role) in status colours; a batch is re-uploaded only when its positions change, and only when the model's objects change. The `flagged` layer draws in `--ez-red` (EZD-204b).
- **Zoom stack (EZD-207b).** `ZoomStack` (`data-testid="zoom-stack"`): Zoom in, Zoom out, Fit to screen at the canvas region's top right, after `plan-input`, for editors and view-only users; absent while loading and when the canvas failed. A step is `ZOOM_STEP_FACTOR` 1.25 about the region centre; at a limit it does nothing. Buttons are 38 px, 44 px under a coarse pointer (`touch.module.css`).
- **Hover (EZD-207b).** `HoverMode` `'wall' | 'off'` (`setHoverMode`, set by the drawing tools). Mouse and pen moves that are not panning pick the wall under the cursor through the EZD-206 wall snap index (one query, `HOVER_CAPTURE_PX` 10 px, the wall's quad); the index is kept current per changed wall (`createHoverIndex().sync`) and rebuilt when the open floor changes. The highlight is one translucent `--ez-cobalt` mesh (opacity 0.35, render order 2.5), uploaded only when the hovered wall or its shape changes; no React commit. `wallAt(screen)` is the same pick for touch. EZD-208a: mode `'select'` hovers what a click would select, or the wall when no picker is set; `getHoveredWallId` answers only for a wall pick. The selection draws in opaque `--ez-cobalt` (order 3.5, lines 3.55), opening bodies in `--ez-cobalt-tint`; hover adds translucent lines at 3.6.
- **Glyph atlas (EZD-207b).** All plan text comes from one 1024 px atlas texture holding both drawing fonts (`ATLAS_CHARS`: U+0020 to U+007E and U+00B7, 64 px cells, 40 px font), drawn once per controller with the fonts named by `--ez-font-mono` and `--ez-font-sans` (no font file URL); only the first family of each variable is loaded and drawn, never the `local(Arial)` fallback faces `next/font` adds (EZD-373). White glyphs on black, read as the text mesh's `alphaMap`; one mesh with vertex colours (`--ez-ink` dimensions, `--ez-slate-300` room labels). Text is model geometry (1 pt = 0.5 model inch), so it scales with the zoom and is never rebuilt on a pan or zoom. A failed atlas is reported as `plan.glyph_atlas` and the plan draws without text; `atlasFailed()` turns true, `subscribeAtlasFailed` listeners are told once, and the editor shows `PlanTextFailedBanner` (`plan-text-failed-banner`, `office.plan.text_failed`) in `editor-state` (EZD-376).
- **Show (EZD-207b).** `showObject(id)` fits the view to that object's drawing-list items (the same point set as Fit); `false` when the object is not on the plan.
- **Test hook.** Ready and `devHooks`: `window.__ezdraftPlan` (`{ handle, stats() }`, stats `frames`, `drawCalls`, `bufferUploads`, `glyphs`, `highlightUploads`, `selectionUploads`, `contexts`), for editors and view-only alike; removed on unmount. Never in production.

### Sign-out and app-wide drain (EZD-120d)

EZD-120d (vault/plans/EZD-120d.md Build steps 1-20, section 3); Q-0503; Q-0507; Q-0581; Q-0048 (a); Q-0505; Q-0508.

- **Drain.** The office layout (`app/src/app/(office)/layout.tsx`, async) loads the signed-in person with `loadOutboxActor()` (`app/src/server/outbox/actor.ts`) and wraps the page in `OutboxDrain` (`app/src/components/outbox/OutboxDrain.tsx`). With an actor and a queued count that is not 0, `app/src/lib/outbox/office-drain.ts` is loaded with `import()` and `startDrain` sends the outbox from any office page; with nothing queued it is never loaded. `countQueued` (`app/src/lib/outbox/device-db.ts`, which also holds `DEVICE_DB`, `OUTBOX_STORE`, `actorRange`) opens the device database with no version and never creates it.
- **Sign-out.** `SignOutButton` and `AvatarMenu` run the caller's check, then `useOutboxSignOut().check()`: queued edits are sent first (`sendBeforeSignOut`, 10,000 ms), and when some stay, the dialog `office.outbox.sign_out_*` asks; the edits are never deleted. The next person to sign in on the device does not send them: a save carries `x-ezdraft-membership` (`MEMBERSHIP_HEADER`) and `POST /api/projects/[id]/objects` answers 401 `not_signed_in` when it is not the session's membership.
- **Signed-out marker** (`app/src/lib/auth/device-sign-out.ts`; Q-0599). The name comes from `signedOutCookieName(secure)`: `__Host-ezd_signed_out` on https (`Path=/; Secure`, no `Domain`, so no subdomain or plain-http page can plant it), `ezd_signed_out` on http (local and test runs). When the sign-out cannot reach the server, `signOutOnDevice` writes this 400-day cookie (the httpOnly session cookies cannot be cleared by script; the browser picks the name by `location.protocol`) and goes to `/sign-in`; a Next redirect error (the real sign-out) is rethrown unchanged. `proxy.ts` picks the name with `isForwardedHttps(request.headers)` from `cookie-names.ts`, the same test as the session jar's `isHttps`, and honours only that name (the other is ignored). A request with the marker is signed out: it deletes every session cookie (`isSessionCookieName`: `sb-…` and `__Host-sb-…`) and the marker (`secure` on https), skips `getClaims`, and answers with `no-store`. The server session is not revoked (Q-0581).
- **Test page.** `/dev/saves/<project id>` (`app/src/app/(editor)/dev/saves/[id]/`, not found in production) and `GET /api/dev/model/[id]` (checkpoint count).

### Company settings (EZD-112)

EZD-112 (vault/plans/EZD-112.md Steps 1-19); S15a; Q-0228; Q-0232; Q-0302; Q-0352 (b).

- **Write path.** `public.update_tenant_identity(p_name, p_license_no, p_contact, p_set_logo, p_logo_path)` (`security definer`, `authenticated` only, in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`) is the only write to `tenant`, which stays `select`-only. It writes the claim tenant's row, checks the caller's own `settings_company` row (`42501` `permission_denied`), refuses an empty name (`22023` `company_name_required`), a contact that is not a JSON object and a logo path outside `<tenant_id>/tenant/logo/<uuid>.<png|jpg|svg>` (`22023`), and returns the row with `changed` (`name`, `logo`, `license_no`, `contact`, in that order); nothing changed means no update, so `updated_at` stays.
- **Routes** (each `requireApiPermission('settings_company')`, `Cache-Control: no-store`): `PATCH /api/settings/company` (`{ name, licenseNo, contact, logo: LogoChange }` → `{ company: CompanyData }`); `POST /api/settings/company/logo` (EZD-295: the raw file bytes with `content-type` `image/png`, `image/jpeg` or `image/svg+xml` → `{ logo: { path, url, kind } }`; the route runs `cleanLogo` and writes the clean bytes to `media` at a new path as the signed-in member); `POST /api/settings/company/logo/check` (`{ path }` → `{ logo: { path, url, kind } }`; a too-large or refused file is removed). Both the check route and the PATCH (for a `set` logo, before the write) run the one check `checkLogoObject` (`app/src/server/company/company.ts`). Codes: `invalid_input`, `company_name_required`, `invalid_email`, `logo_wrong_type`, `logo_too_large`, `logo_svg_unsafe`, `permission_denied`, `db_unavailable`.
- **Logo rules.** PNG, JPG or SVG up to `LOGO_MAX_BYTES` 4,000,000 bytes (`app/src/lib/company/company.ts`); bytes checked by `cleanLogo` (`app/src/server/company/company.ts`; `inspectLogo` is its code or null): PNG and JPG signatures; an SVG with a NUL byte or a UTF-16 byte order mark is refused, otherwise `cleanSvg` (`app/src/server/company/svg-logo.ts`, EZD-290) reads it with a strict XML parser (no entity declarations, only the five named and numeric references; anything it cannot read is `logo_svg_unsafe`), refuses a root that is not SVG (`logo_wrong_type`) and refuses `script`, `foreignObject`, an `on…` attribute or a `javascript:`/`vbscript:` value anywhere, then writes a new file from the parse with only the `SVG_ELEMENTS` and `SVG_ATTRIBUTES` allowlists (links only to `#id`, or a PNG/JPEG `data:` URL on `image`; CSS without `@`, `\` or functions other than `rgb`, `rgba`, `hsl`, `hsla`, `url(#…)`). `checkLogoObject` stores that copy at a new logo path when it differs from the upload, removes the upload and returns the path, which the check route signs and the save route saves; a copy over 4,000,000 bytes is `logo_too_large`. Path `<tenant_id>/tenant/logo/<uuid>.<kind>` in `media`, a new path per upload; old logo files are never deleted. A logo is shown only as `<img src>` of a 900 s signed link, never inline SVG or a `data:` URL.
- **Save timing.** A chosen logo and Remove are staged in the form and stored only by Save; the device draft keeps the staged path (no link), and a restored draft re-checks it through the check route.
- **Event.** `tenant.updated` (`log_only`, payload `{ fields: TenantField[] }`) is emitted by the save route when `changed` is not empty; a failed emit does not fail the save.
- **Shell additions.** `SettingsMain` takes `subtitle` and `flush`; a page adds status bar items after the Company item with `useSettingsStatus(items)` (`SettingsStatusEntry`).

### Customers (EZD-113)

EZD-113 (vault/plans/EZD-113.md Steps 1-18); S15a Customers; 03d customer; Q-0227; Q-0228; Q-0232.

- **Table `public.customer`** (tenant-owned): `name` and `email` required (not blank), `phone` and `address` optional, `archived_at`. Email is unique per tenant ignoring case, archived rows included (`customer_tenant_email_key` on `(tenant_id, lower(email))`), stored trimmed as typed; another company may reuse it. Policies `customer_select`, `customer_insert`, `customer_update` by the claim tenant; `authenticated` has `select, insert, update` only.
- **No delete** (Q-0227): no delete grant or policy for `authenticated`, no `DELETE` route, no Delete control; a customer is archived and restored instead.
- **Project link.** `project.customer_id` (nullable, composite foreign key `project_customer_fk` to `customer (id, tenant_id)`, index `project_customer_id_idx`) is what the project count reads; EZD-118 fills it when the wizard picks a client.
- **List.** `public.list_customers(p_search, p_archived, p_page)` (`security invoker`, `authenticated` only, in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`) returns one page of 50 (`CUSTOMER_PAGE_SIZE`) ordered by `lower(name)`, `id`, with `total`, `active`, `archived` and each row's `projectCount` from one grouped query over the projects the viewer can see (RLS on `project`). Search is a trimmed, case-insensitive substring of name or email, with `%`, `_` and `\` matched literally.
- **Routes** (`Cache-Control: no-store`): `GET /api/settings/customers?q=&archived=0|1&page=` (`CustomerPage`; `requireApiPermission(['settings_company', 'create_projects'])`, and `archived=1` without Company settings is 403 `permission_denied`, Q-0400, Q-0414, EZD-118); `POST /api/settings/customers` (the same any-of check) (`{ name, email, phone, address }` → 201 `{ customer }`); `PATCH /api/settings/customers/<id>` (`requireApiPermission('settings_company')`; the same body, or `{ archived: boolean }` → 200 `{ customer }`; nothing changed means no write). Codes: `invalid_input`, `customer_name_required`, `customer_email_required`, `invalid_email`, `customer_email_taken`, `customer_not_found`, `permission_denied`, `db_unavailable`. Helpers in `app/src/lib/customers/customers.ts` and `app/src/server/customers/customers.ts`.
- **Events.** `customer.created` `{ customerId }` and `customer.updated` `{ customerId, fields }` (`log_only`; `fields` from `name`, `email`, `phone`, `address`, `archived`); a failed emit does not fail the save.
- **Page.** `CustomersPanel` fills `CompanyForm`'s `company-customers` column and adds the `customers` status bar item (active count); `CustomerDialog` (Radix `Dialog`) saves at once through its route, outside the company form's Save.

### Users page (EZD-104)

EZD-104 (vault/plans/EZD-104.md Build steps 1-20); S15; Q-0695; Q-0706; Q-0718 (a); Q-0719 (a).

- **Files and exports.** `app/src/lib/users/users.ts` (`USER_PAGE_SIZE` 50, `UserStatus`, `UserRole`, `UserRow`, `UserCounts`, `UserPage`, `RoleTone`, `grantedCount`, `isModified`, `roleTone`, `formatLastActive`, `formatUserDate`); `app/src/server/users/users.ts` (`UsersQuery`, `parseUsersQuery`, `listUsers`, `touchLastActive`); `app/src/components/settings/users/UsersPanel.tsx` (`UsersPanel`, `UserInspector`) with `Users.module.css`; `app/src/app/(office)/settings/users/page.tsx`. `SettingsMain` takes `actions` (the head's right side). Migration `20261111120001_create_users_page_functions.sql`.
- **List.** `public.list_users(p_search, p_page)` (`security invoker`, `authenticated` only, in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`) returns one page of 50 `active` and `invited` memberships (active first, then invited; by `lower(name or email)`, then id) with each row's role (`null` without one), granted switch keys (sorted) and times, plus `total` (rows matching the search) and the company counts `active`, `invited`, `en`, `es` (never filtered by the search). Search is a trimmed, case-insensitive substring of name or email, with `%`, `_` and `\` matched literally. `canCopyLink` is true on an invited row when the viewer's active membership holds the Owner preset role (EZD-105a adds the last sender). Deactivated members are not listed (EZD-106 adds them).
- **Last active.** `public.touch_last_active()` (`security definer`, behind `private.require_api_gate()`, `authenticated` only, in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`) moves the caller's own active membership's `last_active_at` when it is null or older than 15 minutes and answers whether it wrote; no claim, an ended session or a membership that is not active writes nothing. `getPermissionContext()` runs it once per request beside `current_permissions` on the same session client; `touchLastActive` never rejects (`42501` ignored, anything else reported as `last_active_touch_failed`, location `permissions.last_active`, no alert).
- **Route.** `GET /api/settings/users?q=&page=` (`requireApiPermission('settings_users')`, `Cache-Control: no-store`) → 200 `UserPage`; `q` over 1,000 characters after trimming or a `page` outside 1 to 999,999 is 400 `invalid_input`; 401 `not_signed_in`, 403 `permission_denied`, 500 `db_unavailable` (reported once).
- **Panel.** `UsersPanel` loads a page per search change (page 1, no debounce) and per pager click (Previous and Next when over 50); Up and Down on the focused table move the one selection (no wrap), and `UserInspector` shows the selected row's head or "Select a user"; the status bar gets `users` ("n active · n invited") and `languages` ("EN n · ES n") after the first load, kept on a failed one; the costs info note sits in `users-footer` under the body in every state. Resend, Copy link and Invite your team have no action yet (EZD-105 parts).

### Projects page (EZD-117a)

EZD-117a (vault/plans/EZD-117a.md Steps 1-15); S01 Controls, Data shown, States; Q-0201; Q-0202; Q-0394; Q-0402; Q-0403.

- **Functions** (`security invoker`, `authenticated` only, in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`; RLS limits `project` to the claim tenant and the viewer's scope; archived projects are never listed or counted): `public.list_projects(p_sort, p_status default null, p_after default null)` returns `{ items, next }`, one keyset page of 48 (`PROJECT_PAGE_SIZE`), the client name from `customer` through `project.customer_id` (`project.client_name` is not read); an unknown sort returns no items. `public.count_projects()` returns the six tab counts (`all` and each status) from one grouped count.
- **Sort orders.** `edited`: `updated_at` desc, `id` desc; `name`: `lower(name)` asc, `id` asc; `client`: projects with a client first, `lower(client)` asc, `id` asc; `status`: Draft, Client review, Permit submitted, Approved, In build, then `updated_at` desc, `id` desc; `open_items`: `open_items_count` desc, `updated_at` desc, `id` desc. Text keys compare their first 500 lower-case characters.
- **Routes** (each `requireApiPermission('view_projects')`, `Cache-Control: no-store` on every response, the guard's 401 included): `GET /api/projects?status=&sort=&after=` (`ProjectPage`; `status` one of `PROJECT_TABS`, absent = `all`; `sort` one of `PROJECT_SORTS`, absent = `edited`; any other or empty value is 400 `invalid_input`) and `GET /api/projects/counts` (`ProjectCounts`); a failed read is 500 `db_unavailable`, reported with the route.
- **Cursor.** `next` is the base64url of the last row's sort keys as JSON (`encodeCursor`); `decodeCursor` refuses over 8,200 characters, characters outside `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`, bad JSON or a shape outside the strict schema. Cast-bounds rule: every cursor the schema accepts passes PostgreSQL's `::uuid`, `::timestamptz`, `::boolean`, `::integer` casts and text comparison in `list_projects`, so a forged cursor is a 400, never an RPC error: `u` at most 32 characters, no year 0000, offset within +-15:59; `n` and `c` well-formed, no U+0000, at most 1,000 characters; `o` 0..2147483647. Opaque to the browser. Helpers in `app/src/lib/projects/projects.ts` and `app/src/server/projects/projects.ts`.
- **Store.** `createProjectListStore(api)` (`app/src/lib/projects/projects-store.ts`, `projectHttpApi` over `fetch`): load = one page request and one counts request in parallel; a tab or sort change = one page request (counts unchanged); scrolling = the next page by `after`. A change aborts the page and more loads; a response from an older load is dropped; an `AbortError` changes nothing.
- **Page.** `/` is `app/src/app/(office)/page.tsx`: `ProjectsView` (title row with the status tabs and counts, the sort menu on EZD-108's `Menu`, never Radix `DropdownMenu`, then the 4-column cards and a sentinel that loads the next page within 400 px of the view), or `ProjectsNoAccessView` without View projects. Cards have an empty thumbnail slot; each card is a link to the editor, `/projects/<id>` with `prefetch={false}` (EZD-120c). Loading, error and empty states are EZD-117b's: the grid stays empty.
- **Test ids.** `projects-title`, `projects-tab-<tab>` (`aria-pressed`), `projects-tab-count`, `projects-sort`, `projects-sort-menu`, `projects-sort-<sort>`, `projects-grid` (`data-state`), `projects-more` (`data-more-state`); card `project-card` (`data-project-id`, `data-status`), `project-card-thumb`, `project-card-name`, `project-card-status` (`data-status`), `project-card-place`, `project-card-version`, `project-card-open` (`data-open-items`, `data-has-items`).
- **Stand-ins.** The Settings link (`home-settings`, EZD-289, only to a user who may open Settings) and Sign out (`sign-out`) sit at the right end of the title row in both views until EZD-117b's header (`AvatarMenu`) replaces them.

EZD-117b (vault/plans/EZD-117b.md Build steps 1-13; Q-0401; Q-0402; Q-0448): both routes take `q`, the search (trimmed; over 1,000 characters, `PROJECT_SEARCH_MAX`, is 400 `invalid_input`); an empty search sends no `q` and no `p_search`, so the unsearched requests are unchanged. `list_projects` and `count_projects` take `p_search text default ''`: a case-insensitive substring of the project name, address or client (`customer.name`), with `%`, `_` and `\` literal; the counts follow the search, never the tab. The header (`ProjectsHeader.tsx`: wordmark, title, search field, `NotificationBell`, the settings gear `projects-settings` to a user who may open Settings, `AvatarMenu`; no EZ Binder link) sits above the board in both views; without View projects it has no search field, no bell and no key listener. "/" focuses the search unless a text entry has focus (`isSearchShortcut`); Esc clears it. The store debounces typing by `SEARCH_DEBOUNCE_MS` (250 ms) and adds `setQuery`, `clearSearch`, `clearFilters` and `retry`. States: `OfflineBanner`, `projects-error` (page or counts failed, Retry), six `projects-skeleton` cards while loading, `projects-empty` (New project with Create projects), `projects-no-match` (Clear filter), `projects-more-error`. The Settings link and Sign out stand-ins are removed: the gear and the avatar menu replace them.

### Plan thumbnail (EZD-224)

EZD-224 (vault/plans/EZD-224.md Build steps 1-14); S01 Card thumbnail; Q-0664; Q-0403.

- **Functions** (definer, `authenticated` only, in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`): `start_checkpoint_thumbnail(p_project, p_version)` returns the path; `set_checkpoint_thumbnail(p_project, p_version)` locks the project's `edit_lock` row, re-checks, requires one `image/webp` object of 1 to 40,959 bytes owned by the caller, and sets `model_version.preview_path` and `project.thumbnail_path`. Both use `private.checkpoint_thumbnail_refusal`: the caller's own newest checkpoint with no preview, Edit model, project in scope.
- **Routes** (`Cache-Control: no-store`): `POST /api/projects/[id]/versions/[versionId]/thumbnail` (200 `{ upload }`) and `…/thumbnail/complete` (200 `{ thumbnail: { versionId } }`, 409 `thumbnail_invalid`), both Edit model; `GET /api/projects/[id]/thumbnail` (View projects) answers 302 to a 900 s signed link or 404 `thumbnail_not_found`.
- **Card**: `ProjectSummary.hasThumbnail` comes from `list_projects`; the card shows a lazy 220 x 128 `img` (`project-card-thumb-img`) from `projectThumbnailSrc(id)`, else the empty slot. Capture and encode are EZD-224b.

### New project wizard (EZD-118)

EZD-118 (vault/plans/EZD-118.md Build steps 1-17); S01 New project wizard; 03b rule 1; Q-0394; Q-0396; Q-0398; Q-0399; Q-0400; Q-0403; Q-0410; Q-0411; Q-0413; Q-0451.

- **Two steps since EZD-221b** (see Blank templates). New project (`projects-new`, `projects-empty-new`, no menu) opens `NewProjectDialog` (`app/src/components/projects/NewProjectDialog.tsx`, Radix `Dialog`): Customer, Project name and Type, Address, Scope of work, then Create project. No code edition, submittal profile or EZ Binder fields (Q-0411, Q-0398, Q-0399, Q-0401). The type fills the name (`{type} remodel`) until the name is typed in; Create needs name, address and type and a connection. Cancel, close, Esc and an overlay click close an untouched dialog at once and ask Discard or Keep editing (`useConfirm`) once anything was entered. Helpers in `app/src/lib/projects/new-project.ts`.
- **Function** `public.create_project(p_name, p_address, p_type, p_customer_id, p_scope_of_work) returns table (project_id, tenant_id)` (`security definer`, `authenticated` only, in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`) is the only project write path: the Create projects check for the session's `membership_id` claim (never a parameter), `status` `draft`, `settings` `{}`, `code_edition` and `submittal_profile_id` null, and one `membership_project` row for a `listed` member in the same transaction. Errors 42501 `permission_denied`, 22023 `invalid_input` and `customer_unavailable` (an archived or other company's customer).
- **Route** `POST /api/projects` (`requireApiPermission('create_projects')`, `Cache-Control: no-store`): body `{ name, address, type, customerId, scopeOfWork }` (`parseNewProject`) → 201 `{ project: { id } }`; 400 `invalid_input` or `customer_unavailable` (`error.project_customer_unavailable`), 401, 403, 500 `db_unavailable`. Emits `project.created` `{ projectId, source: 'standalone' }` (`log_only`); a failed emit does not fail the create.
- **Customer picker** `CustomerPicker` (Radix `Popover`): the active customers through `GET /api/settings/customers` (open, then a debounced search, 50 a page with Show more), its own loading, error, empty and no-match states; "add new" opens EZD-113's `CustomerDialog` in create mode, whose `onDone(saved)` picks the new customer.
- **After create** the dialog closes and `ProjectListStore.reset()` returns the list to All, Last edited and no search, loading a fresh page and counts, so the new Draft card is first (Q-0394).
- **Test ids.** Dialog `new-project-dialog`, `new-project-title`, `new-project-close`, `new-project-customer`, `new-project-customer-add`, `new-project-name`, `new-project-type`, `new-project-address`, `new-project-scope`, `new-project-error`, `new-project-ready`, `new-project-cancel`, `new-project-create`; picker `new-project-customer-menu`, `new-project-customer-search`, `new-project-customer-none`, `new-project-customer-option` (`data-customer-id`), `new-project-customer-line`, `new-project-customer-loading`, `new-project-customer-load-error`, `new-project-customer-empty`, `new-project-customer-no-match`, `new-project-customer-more`.

### Blank templates (EZD-221)

EZD-221a (vault/plans/EZD-221a.md Build steps 1-8); S01 Template picker; 03b rule 1; Q-0565; Q-0566; Q-0568; Q-0570.

- **Function** `public.create_project_with_objects(p_name, p_address, p_type, p_customer_id, p_scope_of_work, p_objects jsonb) returns table (project_id, tenant_id)` (`security definer`, `authenticated` only, in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`): Create projects only, not Edit model (Q-0566). It creates the project through `public.create_project`, which stays the one project write path, then inserts the template's `model_object` rows (1 to 5 objects `{ id, type, schema_version, data }`, type `wall` or `room`, ids unique) at seq 1 with each `data.level` set to the new project's floor, in the same transaction; any error rolls back the whole call. Errors: the gate, then `create_project`'s (42501 `permission_denied`, 22023 `invalid_input` / `customer_unavailable`), then 22023 `invalid_input` for `p_objects`.
- **Templates** `app/src/lib/projects/templates.ts` (`TEMPLATE_KEYS`, `TEMPLATE_DEFAULTS`, `DEFAULT_CEILING_HEIGHT_IN`, `TEMPLATE_SIZE_MAX_IN`, `TEMPLATE_ROOM_NAMES`, `TemplateBody`, `isTemplateSize`, `templateAreaSqFt`) is geometry-free (no React, zod, Supabase or `@ezdraft/geometry`), because EZD-221b's browser code imports it.
- **Objects** `buildTemplateObjects(t, newId?)` (`app/src/server/projects/template-objects.ts`): four walls of the W × L rectangle (thickness outside the room, height = Ceiling height), then one room named after the template; `level` is `TEMPLATE_LEVEL_PLACEHOLDER` until the function replaces it.
- **Route** `POST /api/projects` body adds optional `template: { key, width, length, ceilingHeight } | null` (inches); a bad template → 400 `invalid_input`. With a template `createProject` calls `create_project_with_objects`, without one `create_project`.
- **Two steps** (EZD-221b; vault/plans/EZD-221b.md Decided "Steps"; Q-0590 (a)). Step 1 is the EZD-118 dialog with Next (needs name, address and type); step 2 (`NewProjectStep2`, `app/src/components/projects/NewProjectStep2.tsx`) is the wide dialog: a recap of step 1 with Edit, the Blank (template) source with the template picker, and the units and what Create will do; its footer has Back and Create. Back and Edit return to step 1 with every value kept; a failed Create stays on step 2. Create sends `template` (`null` for Empty).
- **Lazy picker** `app/src/lazy/template-picker/` (`TemplatePicker`, the pure `template-draft.ts` helpers), reached through `templateModule` (`app/src/components/projects/template-module.ts`), because only it imports `@ezdraft/geometry` (`parseLength`, `formatLength`) and the Projects entry stays geometry-free; step 2 shows its own loading and an inline error with Retry.
- **Empty hint** `EmptyPlanHint` (`app/src/editor/EmptyPlanHint.tsx`, `hasNoWalls`): over a ready plan with no wall, a user with Edit model sees `office.plan.empty_hint`; it reads the model store, so it leaves with the first wall.
- **Test ids.** Wizard `new-project-dialog` (`data-step` `1`/`2`), `new-project-step`, `new-project-next`, `new-project-back`, `new-project-steps`, `new-project-recap`, `new-project-recap-edit`, `new-project-source-blank`, `new-project-template-block` (`data-state` `loading`/`error`/`ready`), `new-project-template` (`data-template`, `data-selected`), `new-project-template-width`, `-length`, `-ceiling`, `new-project-template-area`, `new-project-template-accepts`, `new-project-template-error`, `new-project-units`, `new-project-create-will`; editor `plan-empty-hint`.

### Notifications (EZD-110)

EZD-110a (vault/plans/EZD-110a.md Steps 1-22); EZD-110b (the bell); 04 Notifications; S01 Notifications button; Q-0403; Q-0413; Q-0422; Q-0425; Q-0121 (a).

- **Table `public.notification`** (one row per notification for one person): `tenant_id`, `user_id`, `kind` (the nine of `NOTIFICATION_KINDS` in `app/src/lib/notifications/kinds.ts`, same order), `project_id` (indexed, **no foreign key**, so a notification keeps its project id after the project is deleted: AC-EZD-110-7, D3), `payload` (the title params: a JSON object of at most 4,096 bytes; `edit_request` needs a string `name`, `client_response` an `action` of `approved` or `commented` and a string `version`), `link` (a relative path of at most 2,048 characters with no `//`, `/\`, spaces or control characters, so it is never an open redirect; phase 2 stores `/`, D2), `read_at`, `emailed_at`, `created_at`. Policy `notification_select`: the claim tenant, the caller's own `user_id`, and the project scope clause for rows with a project; plus `notification_api_gate`. `authenticated` has `select` only (`TABLE_GRANTS`), `service_role` all four; no insert, update or delete policy.
- **Functions.** `private.create_notification(tenant, user, kind, project, payload, link) → uuid` is the one writer (no API role may execute it; later kinds call it from triggers): it returns null and inserts nothing when the person has no `active` membership in the tenant, the project is not in the tenant, or a `listed` membership does not list it; otherwise it inserts the row and one `notification_email` job (tenant-level, `max_attempts` 5) in the same transaction (D4). `public.list_notifications(before_created_at, before_id) → jsonb` (`security invoker`: 20 per page by `created_at desc, id desc`, `unreadCount` from the partial index, `hasMore`, project and client names through RLS), `public.open_notification(id) → table (link, project_id, project_state)` (marks the caller's own row read once; `project_state` `none`, `visible` or `gone`) and `public.mark_all_notifications_read() → integer`, each `authenticated` only and in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`; the definer ones call `private.require_api_gate()` first. `send_test_notification(membership, kind, project, payload)` is `service_role` only, called by the dev route (EZD-323).
- **Events** on `user:<app_user_id>`: `notification.created` `{ notificationId }` (insert trigger `notification_broadcast_created`) and `notification.read` `{ notificationId }` (open) or `{ notificationId: null }` (mark all). Ids only; the bell refetches its list on every event, so counts never come from a payload (D13). A failed send never rolls back the write.
- **Email job** `notification_email` (`app/src/server/jobs/handlers/notification-email.ts`, production included): payload `{ notificationId }`, result `{ emailed }`. Notification missing or the person without an `active` membership: no send, `{ emailed: false }`; `emailed_at` set: no send, `{ emailed: true }`. Else one English system email (no `company`): subject and first paragraph the title, then the project line when the project exists and the membership may see it (`{project} · {client}`, the client from `project.customer_id`), and the link `appOrigin` + the stored link labelled "Open EZ Draft"; idempotency key `notification_email:<job id>`; a failed send is retried by the queue (`JobError` with the send's code). No sender screen: the email's link is the item's stored link (D26, ARCH Email Copy link).
- **Routes** (each `requireApiPermission('view_projects')` first, `Cache-Control: no-store`, `500 db_unavailable` through `reportRouteFailure`): `GET /api/notifications?before=&beforeId=` (both absent, or an ISO date-time with offset and a UUID; else `400 invalid_input`) → `NotificationPage`; `POST /api/notifications/<id>/open` → `200 { link }`, `404 notification_not_found` (bad id or no row), `404 project_gone` (`error.job_not_found`), `403 permission_denied` for a project out of the caller's scope; `POST /api/notifications/read-all` → `200 { marked }`. Server helpers in `app/src/server/notifications/notifications.ts`.
- **Test page** `/dev/notifications` (not found in production): one button per kind (`dev-notify-<kind>`) posting `POST /api/dev/notifications` (`{ kind, projectId?, params }`, params checked by `NOTIFICATION_PARAM_SCHEMAS` (`app/src/lib/notifications/param-schemas.ts`; `kinds.ts` imports no zod, so the bell brings none into the browser, EZD-332); production bare 404; `404 project_not_found` for a project the caller cannot see; `201 { notification: { id, link } }`, then `kickJobRunner()`), and `dev-notify-result`, `dev-notify-link`; `?project=<id>` passes the project.
- `sync_conflict` params (EZD-299a): `{ count, names, more }` strings (`count` 1 to 9999, `names` 1 to 4,096 characters, `more` 0 to 9999); title "Couldn't save {count} changes: {names}" (plural), with " and {more} more" when `more` is above 0 (`### Sync conflicts (EZD-299a)`).
- Bell and list (EZD-110b): `NotificationBell` in `app/src/components/notifications/NotificationBell.tsx`; the list `NotificationPanelView` is the lazy module `app/src/lazy/notification-panel/`, loaded through `lazyModule` when the bell is first opened (skeleton while it loads, `office.notifications.load_failed` with Retry when the download fails; Realtime and the store still load with the page; EZD-332) (Radix `Popover` from `radix-ui`, in a Portal, z-index 90); store `createNotificationStore` and `notificationHttpApi` in `app/src/lib/notifications/store.ts`; times `formatNotificationTime` in `time.ts`; `notificationIcon` in `kinds.ts`.
- The bell refetches on `notification.created`, `notification.read` and every resync of its user channel, the first join included (EZD-318), never on a timer; counts never come from a payload (D13, D16). It needs `MessagesProvider` (office) and `StateHost` above it; EZD-117b mounts it in the Projects header.

### Notification settings (EZD-140)

EZD-140 (vault/plans/EZD-140.md Steps 1-14); Q-0220; Q-0393; Q-0453 (a); S15 line 17.

- **Table `public.notification_setting`** (D1): one row per (tenant, user, kind), primary key `(tenant_id, user_id, kind)` (its first column is the tenant index), `in_app` and `email` (both default true), `updated_at`. No row means both on; settings are per company, as notifications are. Policy `notification_setting_select`: the claim tenant and the caller's own `user_id`; plus `notification_setting_api_gate`. `authenticated` has `select` only (`TABLE_GRANTS`), `service_role` all four; no insert, update or delete policy.
- **Function** `public.save_my_notification_settings(p_settings jsonb) → void` (D2), the only writer: gate first; no tenant or user `42501`; anything but all nine kinds as `{ kind, in_app, email }` with boolean switches and no kind twice is `22023`; upserts and moves `updated_at` only on a row whose switches changed. `authenticated` only, in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`.
- **Notifications follow the settings** (`apply_notification_settings` redefines EZD-110a objects): `notification.in_app` (default true); `private.create_notification` reads the kind's setting (no row: both on) and stores the row with `in_app` from it (D3) and queues the `notification_email` job only when Email is on (D4); a row with `in_app = false` fires no `notification.created` (trigger `when (new.in_app)`), and `notification_select`, `open_notification` and `mark_all_notifications_read` skip it, so `list_notifications` (invoker) leaves it out of items, `hasMore` and `unreadCount`. Rows already stored keep their `in_app`. Both off is allowed (D6): the row is stored hidden and no job. The email job checks the setting again after its `emailed_at` check: Email off → `{ emailed: false }`, no send (D5).
- **Routes** `GET` and `PUT /api/profile/notifications` (D7): `requireApiSession()` first (401 `not_signed_in`), no permission switch (`WRITE_PERMISSION_EXEMPTIONS`); no tenant claim → 403 `permission_denied`; PUT bad JSON or body → 400 `invalid_input`, RPC `42501` → 403, `22023` → 400; 200 `{ settings }` (the nine in `NOTIFICATION_KINDS` order, read again after a save); any other error 500 `db_unavailable` through `reportRouteFailure`; `Cache-Control: no-store`; no event. Helpers: `app/src/lib/notifications/settings.ts` (types, `mergeNotificationSettings`, `parseNotificationSettingsInput`, `settingsChanged`, the fetch helpers) and `app/src/server/notifications/settings.ts`.
- **UI**: My profile's Notifications tab (`NotificationSettingsTab` in the `my-profile` lazy module, Radix `Switch`), loaded when first shown; one footer saves both tabs, the profile first (`### My profile (EZD-108)`).

### Model working copy (EZD-120a)

EZD-120a (vault/plans/EZD-120a.md Build steps 1-10); 03b rules 1, 5, 6, 7, 9; 03c model_version; 03d; 03e; 00d; Q-0507 gap 16; Q-0508; Q-0530; Q-0540.

- **Tables** (each with `tenant_id`, `project_id`, the tenant index, the EZD-006 select policy and `<table>_api_gate`; `authenticated` has `select` only (`TABLE_GRANTS`), `service_role` all four; no write policy): `public.edit_lock` (primary key `project_id`; `lock_epoch` default 0, made at a project's first save or checkpoint; EZD-125 adds the holder), `public.model_object` (primary key `(project_id, object_id)`; `type`, `data` object, `schema_version`, `updated_seq` per object, `updated_by`, `deleted` (soft delete)) and `public.model_version` (a checkpoint: `model_json` `{ objects: [{ id, type, schema_version, data }] }` by id, `parent_version_id` = the project's newest version, `created_at` from `clock_timestamp()`, listed by `(project_id, created_at desc, id desc)`). Trigger `model_version_immutable` refuses (55000) a change to the id, tenant, project, document, `schema_version`, `created_by` or `created_at`, or a `parent_version_id` set to non-null; the other columns stay editable.
- **First floor**: trigger `project_first_floor` (definer) gives every new project one `model_object` of type `floor`, `data` `{ name: 'Floor 1', order: 1 }`, at seq 1; floors are objects like any other (Q-0508; Q-0530).
- **Functions** (`authenticated` only, in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`): `public.save_objects(p_project, p_lock_epoch, p_changes) → jsonb`, the only object writer (except `create_project_with_objects`, which writes a new project's template objects, EZD-221a); the first failing step decides: gate, no claim 42501, no granted `edit_model` 42501, project missing or out of scope P0002, input outside the `parseSaveRequest` rules 22023; then the `edit_lock` row is locked; a different generation answers `{ status: 'stale_lock_epoch' }`, any `base_seq` that differs from the stored seq (0 for no row) answers `{ status: 'conflict', conflicts: [{ object_id, server_seq }] }` for the whole save, neither writing an object; else new objects at seq 1, the rest +1, `{ status: 'saved', tenant_id, objects: [{ object_id, updated_seq }] }`. No count cap. `public.create_checkpoint(p_project) → uuid` runs the same first four checks and calls `private.make_checkpoint(p_project, p_actor)` (invoker, for later definer callers), which copies the live objects under the same lock.
- **Server and routes**: `app/src/server/model/model.ts` (`ObjectChange`, `SaveRequest`, `SaveResult`, `CheckpointResult`, `parseSaveRequest` (zod; a repeated id is refused), `saveObjects`, `createCheckpoint`). `POST /api/projects/[id]/objects` and `POST /api/projects/[id]/checkpoints` (`force-dynamic`): `requireApiPermission('edit_model')` (401, 403), a malformed id or a project out of scope 404 `project_not_found`, bad JSON or body 400 `invalid_input`; objects 200 `{ objects: [{ id, updatedSeq }] }`, 409 `stale_lock_epoch` or `save_conflict` with `conflicts: [{ id, serverSeq }]`; checkpoints 201 `{ version: { id } }`; other errors 500 `db_unavailable` through `reportRouteFailure`; `Cache-Control: no-store`. A save emits `model.saved` `{ objectIds }` (`internal`); a failed emit is reported as `model_saved_event_failed` and the save still answers 200. A room whose closure override is new or changed must name the saving user (`override_other_user`, 400 `invalid_object`); the stored rows are read for those rooms only (EZD-205b).
- **App version and resolves** (EZD-299a): after the body is parsed, a save whose `x-ezdraft-app-build` header is missing, not a number or lower than the server's build is 409 `app_outdated` (`error.app_outdated`) with the server's build in `x-ezdraft-app-build`, nothing written and no event (`### App version (EZD-299a)`). A save may name `resolves` (1 to `changes.length` UUIDs, no repeats, case-insensitive; else 400 `invalid_input`); it then goes to `public.save_objects_resolving` and the 200 body adds `resolved` (`### Sync conflicts (EZD-299a)`).
- **Object numbers** (EZD-294; Q-0625 (a); Q-0629 (b)): `data.attributes.number` on walls and openings, per project and key (`wall`, `door`, `window`), never reused; numbered by `numberNewObjects` from `ModelState.numberHighs` (load: live and deleted rows) in `wallEditChanges`; trigger `model_object_number` keeps a stored number (1 to 2147483647) on update and gives a new object an unused one (index `model_object_number_idx`); `save_objects` answers it and `applySavedNumbers` puts it in the open model; `private.backfill_object_numbers` numbered earlier rows once; `service_role` may execute `object_number_key` and `object_number` because the index evaluates them as the writing role (`PRIVATE_EXECUTE`). When highest + 1 is above 2147483647 the trigger gives the lowest number from 1 that no row of the key holds, deleted rows counted, and refuses with 22023 only when none is free; the schema caps `number` at `NUMBER_MAX` (2147483647) and `numberNewObjects` leaves a new object unnumbered once its key's high is 2147483647, so the server numbers it (EZD-366; D-099).

### Sync conflicts (EZD-299a)

EZD-299a (vault/plans/EZD-299a.md Build steps 1-19); 03b rules 1, 9, 12; 04 Notifications; Q-0502; Q-0511; Q-0531; Q-0540; Q-0627; Q-0628; Q-0632 (a).

- **Table `public.sync_conflict`** (a change the server refused, kept for the person who made it; one row per person, project and object, unique index `sync_conflict_project_user_object_key`): `tenant_id`, `user_id` (app_user, cascade), `project_id` (foreign key `(project_id, tenant_id)` to `project`, cascade, so a project delete removes its rows), `object_id`, `reason` (`conflict`, `invalid` or `not_editable`), `base_seq`, `deleted`, and mine as sent: `type`, `schema_version`, `data` (all null for a delete, all set otherwise), `created_at`, `updated_at`. No project text and no saved value is stored. Policy `sync_conflict_select`: the EZD-006 project template and `user_id = (select private.app_user_id())`; plus `sync_conflict_api_gate`. `authenticated` has `select` only (`TABLE_GRANTS`), `service_role` all four; no write policy.
- **Functions** (definer, gate first, no tenant or user claim 42501; `authenticated` only, in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`): `public.record_sync_conflicts(p_project, p_changes, p_payload) → jsonb` (no membership claim 42501; project not visible now P0002; 1 to 1,000 strict changes, `data` at most 131,072 bytes, no id twice, and the payload exactly `{ count, names, more }` strings with `count` the number of changes, else 22023; upserts each change in order, keeping a row's id and `created_at`, then one `private.create_notification` of kind `sync_conflict`; returns `{ ids, notification_id }`); `public.list_my_sync_conflicts() → jsonb` (the caller's rows in every project, `updated_at desc, id desc`, as one array; the reason read is `not_open` when the project is no longer visible, else `not_editable` without a granted Edit model switch, else the stored one; for a not-open row project name, client name, mine, saved, saved by and saved at are null; `saved` is the `model_object` row, `saved_at` its `updated_at`, `saved_by` the name of its `updated_by` only when that person has a membership in the tenant); `public.delete_my_sync_conflict(p_id) → boolean` (the caller's own row, no scope check); `public.save_objects_resolving(p_project, p_lock_epoch, p_changes, p_resolves) → jsonb` (`save_objects` with all its checks; on a save, a bad `p_resolves` is 22023 and rolls the save back, else the caller's named rows of the saved objects are deleted and `resolved` (sorted ids) is added).
- **Routes** (`force-dynamic`, `Cache-Control: no-store`, `{ error: { code, message_key } }`, any throw 500 `db_unavailable` through `reportRouteFailure`; RPC 42501 403 `permission_denied`, P0002 404 `project_not_found`, 22023 400 `invalid_input`): `POST /api/projects/<id>/sync-conflicts` (a membership header that is not the session's 401 `not_signed_in`; `requireApiPermission('view_projects')`; malformed id or out of scope 404; bad JSON or body 400; 201 `{ ids, notificationId }`, then `kickJobRunner()` when a notification was made); `GET /api/sync-conflicts` (200 `{ items: SyncConflictItem[] }`; a saved value that is not deleted is read with `parseStoredObject`); `DELETE /api/sync-conflicts/<conflictId>` (malformed id or no row 404 `sync_conflict_not_found`; 200 `{ removed: true }`). Helpers: `app/src/lib/conflicts/conflicts.ts` (types, `MAX_RECORD_CHANGES` 1,000, `MAX_RECORD_DATA_BYTES` 65,536, `CONFLICT_LIST_QUERY`, `objectLabel`) and `app/src/server/conflicts/conflicts.ts` (`parseRecordRequest`, `fitConflictNames`, `labelsFor`, `recordConflicts`, `listConflicts`, `deleteConflict`).
- **Labels and fit**: `objectLabel` reads the raw value: a wall, door or window with a positive `attributes.number` is "Wall 4", else "Wall"; a room's `attributes.name` and a floor's `name` as text, else "Room" or "Floor"; anything else "Object". An upsert is labelled from its own value, a delete from the stored row (read 100 ids at a time). Each label is cut to 100 code points; labels are joined with `, ` in change order while the JSON of `{ count, names, more }` stays within 4,080 bytes; `more` counts the rest.
- **Link**: the notification's link is `/projects/<projectId>?conflicts=open` (EZD-299c opens the list from it).

### App version (EZD-299a)

EZD-299a (vault/plans/EZD-299a.md Build steps 4, 5, 9, 14; Decided D-Build, D-Order); Q-0626.

- `app/src/lib/app-version.ts` (browser and server): `APP_BUILD_HEADER` (`x-ezdraft-app-build`), `appBuild()` (`EZD_APP_BUILD` read per call; a match of `^[1-9][0-9]{0,14}$` is that number, anything else 0) and `isOlderBuild(header, current)` (true when the header is missing, not `^[0-9]{1,15}$` or lower; a higher one is accepted, a server rolled back). EZD-134 reuses the module.
- `app/next.config.ts` `env` sets `EZD_APP_BUILD` to the build's Unix time in seconds and inlines it into the browser and server bundles; Vitest leaves it unset (0).
- Every save from the outbox sends the header; the objects route answers an older one with 409 `app_outdated` and its own build in the same header. Roll back only with Vercel Instant Rollback (`docs/runbook.md` `### Rollback`): a redeploy of old code makes a newer build number.

### Model schema (EZD-202)

EZD-202 (vault/plans/EZD-202.md Build steps 1-16); 03f Objects inside model_json; 03g; 03b rule 5; Q-0508; Q-0530; Q-0540; Q-0575.
