- **Files** (`packages/geometry/src/`, exported from the package root): `schema/common.ts` (`pointSchema` in inches, `type Point`, `OBJECT_STATUSES` `existing`/`demo`/`new`, `objectStatusSchema`, `levelSchema` = a floor's uuid), one file per object type with its `<type>DataSchema` and `type <Type>Data` (`floor.ts` flat `{ name, order }`, `wall.ts`, `opening.ts` (version 4, EZD-204a: `axis`, `swing`, `hand`, `impactRating`, `shutterType`, `roughOpeningMode`), `room.ts` (holds `status` and `closureOverride`; version 3, EZD-205a); every object strict, so an unknown key is refused), `schema/migrate.ts` (`CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION`, `MIGRATIONS`, `migrateStoredObject`, `migrationChainProblems`), `schema/index.ts` (`OBJECT_TYPES`, `StoredObject`, `ModelObject`, `ObjectIssue`, `parseStoredObject`, `parseModelDocument`), `schema/samples/v<n>.json` (one stored document per version, never edited after merge) and `tolerance.ts` (`COMPARISON_TOLERANCE_IN` 1/64", `coordsEqual`, `pointsEqual`; the schema never rounds). The app takes the package as `workspace:*` and builds it through `transpilePackages`.
- **Version rule**: a change that keeps every stored object valid (a new optional field, type or enum value) keeps the version; any other raises `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION`, adds `MIGRATIONS[old]` (one step to old + 1, never mutating) and `samples/v<new>.json`. `migrate.test.ts` fails on a missing step or sample.
- **Wall line**: `start`→`end` is the line drawn, the inside finished face; `thicknessSide` puts the thickness `left` or `right` seen walking start→end, or `center` (±thickness/2). Closing a chain changes only `thicknessSide`; a thickness change never moves `start` or `end`.
- **Read rule**: every reader of a `model_object` row or a `model_version.model_json` parses through `parseStoredObject` or `parseModelDocument` (issues `invalid_document`, `invalid_data`, `unknown_type`, `unknown_schema_version`, `duplicate_id`, `level_not_found`, each with the object's id, in object order).
- **Save rule**: `saveObjects` parses every upserted change before `save_objects`; any issue refuses the whole save, 400 `{ error: { code: 'invalid_object', message_key: 'error.invalid_input' }, invalid: [{ id, issue }] }` with no write and no `model.saved`; a parsed object is stored as parsed at `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION`. The deferred constraint trigger `model_object_level_check` (`private.model_object_level_check()`, definer, no grant) refuses at commit, `23503` `level_not_found` with the object's id in `detail`, a live non-floor object whose `data.level` is not a live floor of its project, and a floor deleted or retyped while a live object names it; a floor added, restored or deleted with its objects in one save passes. `saveObjects` maps it to the same 400.

### Benchmark project (EZD-236)

EZD-236 (vault/plans/EZD-236.md Build steps 1-9); 11 Budgets; Q-0211; Q-0568.

- **Generator** `packages/geometry/src/benchmark/benchmark.ts` (exported from the package root): `generateBenchmarkModel()` returns the same 3,000 stored objects on every call (no randomness, clock or argument), `BENCHMARK_MIX` floor 2, wall 1,516, opening 762 (720 doors, 42 windows), room 720 (two floors, each an 18 × 20 grid of 120" rooms), ids `benchmarkObjectId(n)` = `b0000000-0000-4000-8000-<n as 12 hex digits>`, n 1-3000.
- **Flag** `project.is_benchmark` (default false), at most one per tenant (unique partial index `project_benchmark_tenant_key`). The trigger `project_benchmark_guard` (`private.project_benchmark_guard()`, invoker, no grant) refuses `anon`, `authenticated` and `service_role` setting it on insert or changing it on update, `42501` `benchmark_flag_server_only`; only the definer function below sets it.
- **Function** `public.create_benchmark_project(p_changes jsonb)` (definer, `authenticated` only, in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`): gate, then the System status switch (`42501`), then returns the tenant's benchmark project as is (`created` false) or makes it, replaces its first floor and writes `p_changes` through `save_objects` (Edit model and scope checked there); any error rolls back the whole call.
- **Route** `POST /api/benchmark/project` (`app/src/app/api/benchmark/project/route.ts`, through `ensureBenchmarkProject` in `app/src/server/benchmark/benchmark-project.ts`): System status and Edit model, no body read; 201 `{ project: { id } }` made now, 200 already there, 401, 403 `permission_denied`, 500 `db_unavailable`, `Cache-Control: no-store`. Answers in production.
- **Rule**: every project list or count excludes `is_benchmark` (`list_projects` and `count_projects` add `and not p.is_benchmark`).

### System status (EZD-135)

EZD-135 (vault/plans/EZD-135.md Build steps 9-14); 11 Operations; R26 R12; Q-0664; Q-0672 (b); Q-0225. The page and cards are EZD-135b.

- **Function** `public.system_backup_status()` (`security definer`, `authenticated` only, in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`): gate, then the System status switch (`42501` `permission_denied`; no live session `42501` `session_ended`), then one row per kind from the `backup_run` rows of scope `all`: the latest succeeded `files` and `db_dump` and the latest succeeded or failed `restore_drill`, columns `kind`, `status`, `finished_at`, `bytes_copied`, `location` only. It is how `authenticated` reads `backup_run`, which it cannot select.
- **Index** `job_failed_finished_idx` on `job (tenant_id, finished_at desc) where status = 'failed'`.
- **Route** `GET /api/settings/system-status?part=jobs|webhooks|backups&page=` (`app/src/app/api/settings/system-status/route.ts`; loaders in `app/src/server/system-status/system-status.ts`; types, constants and `parseStatusQuery` in `app/src/lib/system-status/system-status.ts`): `requireApiPermission('settings_system_status')`, then 400 `invalid_input` for a bad query (`page` 1 to 999999); `jobs` is the failed jobs finished in the last 30 days and `webhooks` the `webhook_failed` alerts, each 50 per page newest first through the session client (RLS); `backups` is `{ files, dbDump, restoreDrill }` from the function (403 `permission_denied` when it refuses); 500 `db_unavailable`; `Cache-Control: no-store`.

### Derived values and workers (EZD-235)

EZD-235 (vault/plans/EZD-235.md Build steps 1-9); 11 rules 2 and 3; 11 Where each output is produced; Q-0207; Q-0568; Q-0664. EZD-235b (vault/plans/EZD-235b.md Build steps 1-7).

- **Files.** `packages/geometry/src/derived/derived.ts` (exported from the package root): `createDerivedCache`, `ROOM_AREA` and the types `DerivedDef`, `DerivedSource`, `DerivedCache`, `DerivedStats`, `DerivedObjectType`, `ObjectOf`. `app/src/editor/derived/model-derived.ts`: `createModelDerived(store)` (`ModelDerived`), `objectsSource`.
- **Pull.** A derived value is computed only inside `get`; an edit computes nothing. It is cached per definition name and key with the objects and lists its compute read through the `DerivedSource`, and stays valid while each read object is the same reference and each read list holds the same references in the same order. A source never changes; a changed model is a new source.
- **Counts.** `stats()` gives computes and hits per definition name and the entry count; `prune(source)` drops entries that are no longer valid or that read an object that is gone.
- **Store binding.** `ModelDerived` parses each stored value once per value and id, and makes a new source only when the store's `objects` map changes; it never reads `lastChangedIds`. `roomAreaSqFt(room)` is the stored room's cached area by id, or the given boundary's area when no stored room has that id.
- **Plan.** The plan controller asks for the room areas of the open floor on each rebuild and prunes after it; a pan or zoom rebuilds nothing, so it computes nothing.
- **Worker pool.** `app/src/editor/workers/worker-pool.ts`: `createWorkerPool({ create, size?, report? })` creates a worker only when a job needs one, never at page load, runs one job per worker and queues the rest first in, first out. The default size is the device's logical processors minus one, from 1 to 4 (`defaultPoolSize`, `MAX_POOL_WORKERS`). A worker that errors fails its running job with `worker.crashed`, is ended and reported (`captureBrowserError`, location `editor.worker_pool`); the next job gets a new worker. `dispose()` ends every worker and fails running, queued and later jobs with `worker.disposed`.
- **Worker side.** A pooled worker file calls `serveJobs(self, handlers)` (`app/src/workers/serve-jobs.ts`): one reply per well-formed request (a message without a whole-number `id` of 1 or more gets none), `worker.unknown_job` for a kind with no handler of its own, `worker.job_failed` when the handler throws or rejects. Files in `app/src/workers/` may not import editor code (`eslint-rules/boundaries.mjs` `EDITOR_CODE_IMPORTERS`).
- **Later heavy work.** Clipper booleans, takeoff, load calc, sheet SVG and DXF parsing (11 rule 3) each add a handler in a `*.worker.ts` that calls `serveJobs`, run through `createWorkerPool`. A one-shot worker started for one task and ended when it answers (EZD-224b's thumbnail encoder) does not use the pool.

### Rooms (EZD-205a)

EZD-205a (vault/plans/EZD-205a.md); 09e; 09j; Q-0559; Q-0568; Q-0570.

- **Files** (`packages/geometry/src/rooms/`, exported from the package root): `area.ts` (`ringSignedAreaSqIn`, `outlineAreaSqIn`, `roomAreaSqFt`, `pointInRing`, `interiorPoint`, `sameBoundary`, `roomLabelText`, `formatRoomLabel`), `outlines.ts` (`findRoomOutlines`), `sync.ts` (`reconcileRooms`), `closure.ts` (`findClosures`: closed, gap up to 12", open, per wall chain), `absorb.ts` (`absorbGap`), `override.ts` (`keepOutOfSquare`); `reconcileRooms` keeps an override while its gap is unchanged (EZD-205b).
- **Outlines** run on the walls' inside finished faces.
- **Area** is derived from the outline in whole square feet, never stored.
- **Reconcile**: every wall-changing tool calls `reconcileRooms` so the stored rooms stay matched to the outlines.

### Openings (EZD-204)

EZD-204a (vault/plans/EZD-204a.md); EZD-204b (vault/plans/EZD-204b.md); 03f; Q-0630; Q-0633; Q-0640; Q-0641; Q-0642.

- **Files** (`packages/geometry/src/openings/`, exported from the package root): `opening.ts` (`OpeningObject`, `DEFAULT_DOOR_SIZE`, `DEFAULT_WINDOW_SIZE`, `DEFAULT_WINDOW_SILL_IN`, `autoRoughOpening`, `createOpeningData`, `flipSwing`, `flipHand`, `isOpeningFlagged`, `isVerticalLine`, `leftEndIsLow`); `offsets.ts` (`HostCorner`, `OpeningOffsets`, `PlaceAtOffsetInput`, `PlaceAtOffsetResult`, `hostCorners`, `openingSpan`, `openingOnWall`, `openingOffsets`, `placeAtOffset`); `hosting.ts` (`openingHostUpdates`); `door-symbol.ts` (`DoorFrame`, `DoorSymbol`, `doorSymbolInFrame`, `doorSymbol`: the one door-arc rule `build.ts` draws with) and `placement.ts` (`DOOR_TARGET_PX`, `PRESS_MOVE_PX`, `OpeningFailure`, `swingSideOf`, `OpeningPreviewInput`, `openingPreview`, `withOpeningSize`, `doorTargetAt`, `openingOwnedAt`) (EZD-211a).
- **Flag**: `hostWallId` null is Not on a wall (`isOpeningFlagged`); `axis` is null while hosted and, while flagged, the unit start→end direction of the last host.
- **Drawing.** An opening with `hostWallId` null is drawn on the `flagged` layer along `axis` (+x when null).
- **Rule.** Every wall edit's `wallEditChanges` appends `openingHostUpdates`; EZD-217's delete and paste must call it too.

## Server-only data access and can()

03 rules 1–2, 11; R26 R1, R2, R14. Documented here; implemented by EZD-102 (helper, server-route pattern, UI hook) and EZD-133 (browser-query lint rule, money registry `app/src/server/db/money-fields.ts`).

- Browser code never queries tables. It calls the app's own HTTP routes (Route Handlers under `app/src/app/api/**`) through one fetch helper in `app/src/lib/` that adds the app version to every request (03 rule 12; EZD-134). Reads and writes use Route Handlers, not Server Actions, because the outbox (03 rule 9) and the EZ Binder inbound API need plain HTTP endpoints.
- The two exceptions (03 rule 1): Realtime subscriptions authorized per project scope (EZD-018) and signed uploads straight to Storage (EZD-132).
- Every route: read the session → load the user's membership and permissions in one indexed query per request (EZD-102) → `can()` → query with a Supabase client carrying the user's session, so RLS stays the backstop (03 rule 1; 11 rule 10) → return an explicit field list.
- Contract: `can(ctx, permission, target?) → boolean`. `ctx` is the per-request context: user, active tenant from the token-hook claim, membership status and scope, permission set. `permission` is a typed key from the EZD-101 list (keys are `PermissionKey` in `app/src/lib/permissions/catalog.ts`, EZD-101). `target` is `{ projectId }` when the action is on a project: scoped users pass only for their listed projects; deactivated users always get false (03 Tables membership; EZD-006). A `requirePermission(...)` wrapper turns false into a 403 (EZD-102). The browser `can()` hook (EZD-102) only hides controls; it is never the security check.
- `ctx`'s active tenant is the `tenant_id` claim the token hook sets (EZD-006); `membership_id` and `project_scope` are in the token too, and `app_user_id` names the user's `app_user` row.
- PostgREST refuses any request without the server-only gate header `x-ezd-api-gate`, so a user's access token alone reads and writes nothing through the REST API; only server routes, which call `can()`, reach it (Q-0087; EZD-006). EZD-007's `createSessionClient()` sends the header from the server-only env name `SUPABASE_API_GATE_SECRET`.
- Money (03 rule 2; R26 R2): cost fields are added to a response only when `can(ctx, <See company costs>)`, and sell, markup and total fields only when `can(ctx, <See sell prices>)`, through one serializer per resource in `app/src/server/`. The cost-removal acceptance test is built by EZD-133 (phase 3), and every ticket that adds a money field extends it (03 rule 2).
- Client link (03 rule 11; R26 R14): client pages are served only by server routes that resolve the token to its sent package and return an explicit list of fields with no money tables; files are private and served through short-lived signed links; only a hash of the token is stored; the comment and verify endpoints are rate-limited; the signer check stays as R24 #32, with five attempts per hour.
- The service-role key is used only in job handlers, migrations, seeds and admin scripts, never in request-path routes and never in browser bundles. Exception (EZD-323): `POST /api/dev/notifications` calls the `service_role`-only `send_test_notification` through `createJobsDbClient()`. The render worker uses its own revocable key (03 render_worker).

### Permission enforcement (EZD-102)

EZD-102 (vault/plans/EZD-102.md Steps 1-19); 03b rule 1; 10; 11 rules 10, 11; R24 (13a) "controls a user lacks are hidden"; Q-0202; Q-0214; Q-0232.

- **One query per request.** `public.current_permissions() returns table (membership_id uuid, project_scope text, permission_keys text[], listed_project_ids uuid[])` (`sql`, `stable`, `security invoker`, `authenticated` only, in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`) returns 0 or 1 row: the token's `membership_id` claim while that membership is `active`, its granted `user_permission` keys (primary key `(membership_id, permission_key)`) and its `membership_project` ids (primary key `(membership_id, project_id)`), both sorted. Invoker, so RLS stays the backstop and an ended session raises `42501` in the policy helpers; no row when the claim is missing (no active membership, or two of them).
- **Server exports** `app/src/server/permissions` (`index.ts`): `PermissionContext` `{ appUserId, membershipId, scope: 'all' | 'listed', permissions: ReadonlySet<PermissionKey>, listedProjectIds: ReadonlySet<string> }`; `loadPermissionContext(client, appUserId)` (one `rpc('current_permissions')`, never `.from()`; `42501` or no row is `null`; any other error throws, Q-0168 (b); keys filtered by `isPermissionKey`); `getPermissionContext()` (React `cache()`, once per request, `null` without a session); `permissionList(ctx)` (the sorted keys, `[]` for null); `can(ctx, permission, target?)` (false when `ctx` is null or the key is missing; with `{ projectId }`, false when `scope` is `listed` and the id is not listed, Q-0202 own jobs = listed projects; scope `all` passes and RLS returns no row for another tenant); `requirePermission(ctx, permission, target?)` (403 `permission_denied` / `error.not_authorized` first, then 404 `project_not_found` / `error.project_not_found` for a project out of scope, both `Cache-Control: no-store`, else `null`; key first, so a 403 says nothing about the project); `requireApiPermission(permission, target?)` (`permission` is one key or a non-empty list of which any one suffices, the first held key used for the target check, EZD-118; `requireApiSession()` → `getPermissionContext()` → `requirePermission`; returns `Permitted` `{ session, ctx }` or the `Response`; a thrown context load is the route's 500 `db_unavailable`).
- **Every write is checked.** Every exported `POST`, `PUT`, `PATCH` or `DELETE` of a `route.ts` under `app/src/app/` contains `requireApiPermission(`; a `'use server'` file is never a write path (Route Handlers, not Server Actions). The only exceptions are `WRITE_PERMISSION_EXEMPTIONS` (`app/test-harness/permissions/write-exemptions.ts`: `api/monitoring/route.ts`, `server/auth/actions.ts`, each with its reason); a new entry needs code-reviewer and security-reviewer approval. `write-routes.test.ts` (U8) scans the repo with `uncheckedWrites` (`app/test-harness/permissions/write-routes.ts`).
- **Table policies never read permission rows.** No RLS policy outside the `storage` schema, and no `private.*` helper such a policy calls, reads `user_permission`, `permission_key`, `default_permissions` or `public.role`; a Storage policy may also read the permission switch its owning screen checks, since it is the only check on a direct file fetch (Q-0323 (1); EZD-189). `policies.db.test.ts` (D4) scans `pg_policies` with an empty `search_path` (so names read back schema-qualified) and the helpers' source, and skips `storage` policies and helpers only they call.
- **Own set route** `GET /api/permissions` (`app/src/app/api/permissions/route.ts`, `force-dynamic`): 200 `{ permissions: PermissionKey[] }` sorted (`[]` without an active membership), 401 `not_signed_in`, 500 `db_unavailable` (reported to Sentry, location `api.permissions`), `Cache-Control: no-store`; no `can()`, since a member may always read their own set.
- **Set to the browser once, refreshed on push.** The server page passes `permissionList(ctx)` as `initial` to `PermissionsProvider` (`app/src/lib/permissions/PermissionsProvider.tsx`), which holds a `PermissionStore` (`createPermissionStore(initial, fetchPermissions)`, `app/src/lib/permissions/permission-store.ts`: `get()`, `subscribe(cb)`, `refresh()`; one fetch in flight, calls during it set one pending flag that runs one more fetch after it settles; a success replaces the set and notifies only on a content change; a rejected fetch keeps the set and notifies nobody). The provider takes the hub's user handle and calls `refresh()` on `user_permission.updated` and on a Realtime resync, never on a timer (11 rule 11). `fetchPermissions()` is `fetch('/api/permissions', { cache: 'no-store' })`, `permissions_refused` on any status but 200.
- **Hidden, not disabled.** `useCan()` returns `(permission) => boolean` over `useSyncExternalStore`; `Can { permission; children }` renders the children when allowed, else nothing (Q-0214; R24). Both throw `permissions_provider_missing` outside the provider. The browser checks keys only; the server page decides project scope. The hook only hides controls; the server route is the check.
- **Permission push.** `private.user_permission_broadcast()` (`security definer`) runs from the statement triggers `user_permission_broadcast_insert`, `_update` (`referencing new table as changed`) and `_delete` (`referencing old table as changed`) on `user_permission`, pushing `user_permission.updated` `{}` on `user:<app_user_id>` once per distinct member per statement (`UserChannelEvents['user_permission.updated']` is `Record<string, never>`); a failed send never rolls back the permission write; a membership deleted in the same statement sends nothing (its sessions end anyway).
- **No access in place.** `NoAccess` (`app/src/components/states/NoAccess.tsx`, exported from `app/src/components/states`): `role="status"`, `data-testid="no-access"`, `data-state="no-access"`, the text `common.no_access` ("You don't have access to this. Ask your supervisor.", Q-0214; Spanish in `es`); a page the user may not open shows it in place with HTTP 200 and no redirect (Q-0232). Route 404 bodies use `error.project_not_found` (en and es).
- **Test pages** (not found in production): `/dev/permissions` (`PermissionsProbe`: `perm-controls` with `perm-control-edit_model` and `perm-control-delete_projects` inside `Can`, `perm-keys`, `perm-projects` `li[data-project-id]` or `NoAccess` without View projects, `perm-channel[data-status]` = the user channel's `ChannelStatus`), `/dev/permissions/projects/<id>` (`perm-project[data-project-id]` or `NoAccess`) and `PATCH /api/dev/permissions/projects/<id>` (body `{ name }`; `requireApiPermission('edit_model', { projectId })`, Q-0202 rename = can edit the project; 200 `{ project: { id, name } }`, 400 `invalid_input`, 401, 403, 404; production bare 404).

### Auth and session (EZD-007)

EZD-007a (vault/plans/EZD-007a.md Steps 1, 1b, 2.0, 2.1, 2.5); 02 Users and role presets; 11 Security and audit; R24 #14; R26 R15; Q-0046 (a); Q-0047 (a); Q-0048 (a); Q-0049; Q-0050 (a); Q-0052 (c); Q-0094; Q-0165 (a). The session, linking, cookie, page and message bullets are EZD-007b–f's.

- **Local Auth config** (`supabase/config.toml`, local only; each cloud project gets the same values by hand, `docs/runbook.md` `## Sign-in (EZD-007)`): `[auth.email]` `otp_length = 6` (R26 R15), `otp_expiry = 900` (Q-0049: link and code valid 15 minutes), `enable_confirmations = true` (a password sign-up gets no session without the mailbox), `secure_password_change = true`; `[auth]` `jwt_expiry = 3600` (Q-0052 (c)), `enable_signup = true` (code sign-in creates the auth user and the hook gates it); `[auth.sessions]` `inactivity_timeout = "9600h"` (Q-0048 (a): 400 days unused); `[auth.rate_limit]` `email_sent = 1000` (local only, so tests can send many emails). `minimum_password_length` is EZD-033's (Q-0094).
- **Sign-in email** `supabase/templates/sign-in.html`, set as both `[auth.email.template.magic_link]` and `[auth.email.template.confirmation]` (Auth sends "confirmation" the first time an email signs in and "magic link" after that); subject `Your EZ Draft sign-in link and code / <Spanish>`. One `lang="en"` block then one `lang="es"` block (Q-0047 (a)); the link is `{{ .SiteURL }}/auth/confirm?token_hash={{ .TokenHash }}&type=email` (no code verifier, so it opens on any device) plus the 6-digit `{{ .Token }}`; never `{{ .ConfirmationURL }}`, no image, no tracking.
- **Auth reads `config.toml` only at `supabase start`:** `pnpm db:up` restarts the stack when `supabase/config.toml` or `supabase/templates/*` changed (EZD-166); a `db reset` alone does not apply them.
- **Before-user-created hook** `private.auth_before_user_created(event jsonb)` (`[auth.hook.before_user_created]` `uri = "pg-functions://postgres/private/auth_before_user_created"`; Q-0165 (a)): `security definer`, executable by `supabase_auth_admin` only (revoked from `public`, `anon`, `authenticated`; not in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`, schema `private` is off the REST API). It allows (`{}`) when `lower(event->'user'->>'email')` matches an `app_user.email` with a membership that is `active` or passes `private.invite_is_valid`; everything else, including a missing email, gets `{"error": {"http_code": 403, "message": "sign_in_not_allowed"}}` (fail closed; R24 #14: users are invited in EZ Draft). Observed on the pinned CLI 2.121.0: the hook runs for the public sign-up and code-request paths and **not** for the admin API (`auth.admin.createUser` with the secret key created a user for an unknown email), so test fixtures that need an auth user go through `createAuthUserPastGate` all the same (finding A3) and admin creation stays a server-only path.
- **Invite validity** `private.invite_is_valid(p_status text, p_invited_at timestamptz)`: `invited` and `invited_at` within the last 7 days (Q-0050 (a); the EZD-105 row); a null sent date is invalid. Execute is revoked from `public` and given to no role (on the pinned stack the schema's default privileges did not cover it; EZD-006 T10 checks `anon` can call only `api_request_gate`); the hook and `link_app_user` run as owner. Called by the hook and by EZD-007b's `link_app_user`; EZD-105 may redefine it with the same signature.
- **Unconfirmed-password guard** (Q-0094): trigger `auth_users_password_guard` `before insert or update on auth.users`, function `private.auth_users_password_guard()` (`security definer`, no role may execute it directly). While `email_confirmed_at is null` it sets `encrypted_password` to `null`, the value the pinned Auth reads as "no password" (the stack itself stores a bcrypt hash even for a code-only user, so the guard writes one constant for every unconfirmed row). Confirmed accounts are untouched; EZD-033 and EZD-105b set passwords on confirmed accounts (Q-0095). The header comment of `supabase/migrations/20261020101101_create_unconfirmed_password_guard.sql` still says the pinned Auth stores null for a user without a password; that file is on `main` (Release run 40086272055, 2026-10-24), so it is never edited (`### Release safety (EZD-023a)` M2) and this bullet is the correct note (EZD-298).
- **Linking at sign-in** (EZD-007b, vault/plans/EZD-007b.md Step 1; Q-0050 (a); Q-0043 (a); Q-0099 (b)): `public.link_app_user() returns table (app_user_id uuid, has_access boolean, accepted_membership_id uuid, accepted_tenant_id uuid)`, `plpgsql`, `volatile`, `security definer`, executable by `authenticated` only (`revoke all … from public, anon, service_role; grant execute … to authenticated`). Zero or one row. No row when `auth.uid()` is null, when the session has ended (`private.session_is_live()` false, finding B4), when the auth email is not confirmed (`email_confirmed_at is null`), or when no `app_user` row holds that lower-cased email unlinked or already linked to this auth user; a row linked to another auth user is never re-pointed. It sets `app_user.auth_user_id`, locks the person's memberships `for update`, then: an `active` membership → `(id, true, null, null)` and nothing accepted (one active company per person); else the most recent `private.invite_is_valid` invite (tie: higher `id`) becomes `active` → `(id, true, <membership>, <tenant>)`, every other invite stays `invited`, `invited_at` and `last_active_at` untouched; else `(id, false, null, null)`. Idempotent. EZD-007d's `completeSignIn` calls it right after `verifyOtp`.
- **Current user** (EZD-007b Step 2; Q-0052 (c)): `public.current_app_user_id() returns uuid`, `sql`, `stable`, `security definer`, `authenticated` only (same two statements). Returns null when `private.session_is_live()` is false, else the `app_user.id` whose `auth_user_id = auth.uid()`, or null. It never raises (it does not call the `private` helpers that raise `42501` on an ended session), so a deactivated user's still-valid JWT gets null and routes fail closed at 401. One `auth.sessions` primary-key lookup and one `app_user` unique-index lookup per call. Read by EZD-007c's `getSession()` and EZD-018.
- **Invite acceptance keeps the session** (EZD-007b Step 3; Q-0044 (b)): `private.membership_end_sessions()` is redefined with `create or replace` (same body; trigger and privileges kept) so a `status` change from `invited` to `active` does not end sessions; every other status change, every scope change and every delete still does, and EZD-165 adds `user_id` and `tenant_id` changes (both people on a `user_id` change).
- Both functions are in `GATED_RPC_CALLS` (`app/test-harness/rls/gate.ts`), so the API request gate applies (EZD-006 T22 (c), (j)).
- **Session client** (EZD-007c, vault/plans/EZD-007c.md Steps 2–4; Q-0087 (ii); Q-0048 (a); Q-0052 (c)): server code reaches Supabase for a signed-in person only through `createSessionClient(jar?)` in `app/src/server/auth/supabase-server.ts` (synchronous; `@supabase/ssr` `createServerClient` with the publishable key). Every PostgREST request carries the gate header `API_GATE_HEADER` (`x-ezd-api-gate`) from the server-only `SUPABASE_API_GATE_SECRET`, read per call by `serverGateSecret()` in `app/src/server/db/env.ts` (at least 43 characters, else it throws `api_gate_secret_not_set`, never the value). It never uses the secret key. `SessionCookieJar` is `{ getAll, set, isHttps }`; without a jar the client uses `next/headers`, and EZD-007d's request gate passes its own jar over the request and response cookies. Cookies are the Supabase auth cookies, set only by the server, no `Domain`, named `__Host-sb-<ref>-auth-token[.N]` on https and `sb-…` on http (EZD-342; Q-0600; the adapter renames with `appCookieName`/`readableCookies` from one `jar.isHttps()` answer, so a plain `sb-` cookie on https is not read), `httpOnly`, `sameSite` `lax`, `path` `/`, `secure` on `https` (first value of `x-forwarded-proto`), and 400 days from last use (`SESSION_COOKIE_OPTIONS`, `maxAge` 34560000; a delete keeps `maxAge` 0). Next.js refuses a cookie write from a Server Component, so the default jar drops it; EZD-007d's request gate writes the token refresh before the page renders, and Route Handlers and Server Actions write normally.
- **Session helpers** (`app/src/server/auth/session.ts`): `Session` is `{ authUserId, appUserId, email }`; the signed-in user's own `app_user` row is `session.appUserId` (EZD-026b, EZD-102). `getSession()` (React `cache()`, once per request) returns `null` when the client cannot be built (missing env or secret), when `auth.getClaims()` errors or has no `sub`, or when `current_app_user_id()` returns null (no linked `app_user`, or an ended session; the generated type says `string`, the code reads `string | null`); a failed `current_app_user_id` call rejects (Q-0168 (b)). `requireSession()` redirects to `/sign-in`; `requireApiSession()` returns a 401 `Response` with `{ "error": { "code": "not_signed_in", "message_key": "error.not_signed_in" } }`.
- **Completing a sign-in** (EZD-007d, vault/plans/EZD-007d.md Steps 2, 3; Q-0050 (a); Q-0052 (c)): `completeSignIn(client)` in `app/src/server/auth/complete-sign-in.ts` runs right after `verifyOtp`: `rpc('link_app_user')` (links the `app_user` and accepts the most recently sent valid invite); no row or `has_access` false signs out this device and returns `'no_access'`; an accepted invite emits `user.joined` with `via: 'sign_in'` (an emit failure does not fail the sign-in, the acceptance is committed); then `auth.refreshSession()` once so the token hook adds the tenant claim, and returns `'ok'`. It fails closed: an RPC error or a refresh error signs out this device and throws `link_app_user_failed` or `session_refresh_failed` (500 unexpected, never shown). It logs nothing.
- **Sign-in actions** (EZD-007d Step 3, `app/src/server/auth/actions.ts`, `'use server'`; Q-0167 (a); Q-0169 (a); Q-0174 (a)): they return codes, never text (EZD-007e owns the wording). `requestSignIn(formData)` (field `email`, trimmed and lower-cased) → `{ step: 'code' }`, or `invalid_email` (no Auth call, no cookie) or `too_many_requests` (only the per-IP 429 `over_request_rate_limit`, no cookie). The same answer for every address: accepted, refused by the hook (403) or any other 429 all return `{ step: 'code' }` and set the cookie, so the answer never shows whether an account exists; any other Auth error throws (500 unexpected). `verifySignInCode(formData)` (field `code`, six digits; `email` from the form, else the cookie) and `confirmSignInLink(formData)` (fields `token_hash`, `type`, only `email` accepted) redirect to `/` on success and clear the email cookie; otherwise `code_invalid` / `link_invalid` (a malformed input makes no Auth call), `no_access` or `too_many_requests` (Auth 429; the cookie stays). `signOut()` ends the session on this device only (`scope: 'local'`) and redirects to `/sign-in`, also when the client cannot be built. `setSignInLanguage(formData)` (field `lang`: `es`, anything else `en`) sets the language cookie; it never changes `app_user.language`. Nothing is logged; codes, token hashes and emails never appear in an error.
- **Sign-in cookies** (EZD-007d): `ezd_sign_in_email` (the address being signed in, `maxAge` 900 s, Q-0049) and `ezd_sign_in_lang` (`en` or `es`, `maxAge` 34,560,000 s, Q-0047 (a)); both `httpOnly`, `sameSite` `lax`, `path` `/`, `secure` on `https` (first value of `x-forwarded-proto`), written only by the server. `getSignInLanguage()` (`app/src/server/auth/sign-in-language.ts`) reads `ezd_sign_in_lang` for the signed-out pages only: `es` when the cookie is exactly `es`, else `en` (Q-0029 (a)); signed-in surfaces use `app_user.language`. On https named `__Host-ezd_sign_in_email` and `__Host-ezd_sign_in_lang`; the plain names are not read there (EZD-342). Read, written and cleared only through `app/src/server/auth/request-cookies.ts`; a clear writes `''` with `maxAge` 0 and the same flags.
- **Cookie names** (EZD-342; Q-0600; Q-0599): `app/src/lib/auth/cookie-names.ts` (no imports) holds every app cookie name, `appCookieName(base, secure)`, `readableCookies(all, secure)`, `isSessionCookieName(name)` and the one https test `isForwardedHttps(headers)` (first `x-forwarded-proto` value exactly `https`). `cookie-names.test.ts` N7 fails when another non-test file under `app/src` quotes one of those names, the prefix or `x-forwarded-proto`, calls `await cookies()` outside `supabase-server.ts` and `request-cookies.ts`, assigns `document.cookie` outside `device-sign-out.ts`, or calls `.cookies.set(`/`.cookies.delete(` outside `proxy.ts`. The EZD-342 deploy signs everyone in once; on https the request gate deletes every plain `sb-` cookie a request carries (host-only, `Max-Age=0`, `Secure`, `Path=/`, with `no-store`); plain sign-in cookies are left to expire.
- **Request gate** (EZD-007d Steps 5, 6; P-Step 4; Q-0173): `app/src/proxy.ts` (Next.js 16 `proxy`, matcher every path except `_next/static`, `_next/image` and files with an extension, plus every `/api` path). Pages are deny-by-default: with no session (`auth.getClaims()` error or no `sub`), a path not in `PUBLIC_PATHS` gets a `307` to `/sign-in` with no query string (no `next=`, so no open redirect). Missing Supabase env or a client that cannot be built counts as signed out; the gate never answers 500. It builds the one session client with its own jar over the request and response cookies, so a token refresh rewrites the 400-day auth cookies on use (400 days from last use, Q-0048 (a)); the cookies it writes go on whichever response it returns, with `Cache-Control: private, no-store`. It does not check for an ended session; pages call `requireSession()` and routes `requireApiSession()`. `PUBLIC_PATHS` (`app/src/server/auth/public-paths.ts`) is `/sign-in`, `/auth/confirm`, `/api` (Route Handlers answer 401 themselves), `/dev` (developer pages, 404 in production) and `/terms` (EZD-1707); `isPublicPath(p)` is true for an entry or an entry followed by `/`. A later public page (client link, inbound API) adds its entry in its own commit.
- **Same-origin writes** (EZD-346; Q-0599): `app/src/server/auth/same-origin.ts`, called first in `proxy.ts`, refuses with 403 `cross_origin_refused` every `/api` request whose method is not GET or HEAD unless `Sec-Fetch-Site` is `same-origin` or, when that header is absent, `Origin` equals the request's own origin. Exceptions only by exact path in `SAME_ORIGIN_EXCEPTIONS`, each with a reason (now `/api/monitoring`). `proxy.test.ts` walks every write `route.ts`.
- **Sign-in pages** (EZD-007f, vault/plans/EZD-007f.md Steps 3–10; Q-0047; Q-0039 (c); Q-0170 (b)): `/sign-in` (email, then the 6-digit code; a signed-in visitor goes to `/`; `?e=no_access` shows `error.no_access`) and `/auth/confirm` (verifies only when its button is pressed, never on `GET`; `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` from `app/next.config.ts` `headers()`), both in the device's language with `LanguageSwitch`. "Use a different email" calls `clearSignInEmail()` (`actions.ts`), which clears the sign-in email cookie through `clearAppCookie` (`__Host-ezd_sign_in_email` on https, `ezd_sign_in_email` on http). An action that rejects for any reason other than Next's redirect shows `error.sign_in_failed`. `SignOutButton` (`app/src/components/auth/`) takes an optional `beforeSignOut` hook; when it answers false nothing is submitted (`confirmSignOut`; EZD-134, EZD-108). `/` requires a session and shows only Sign out until Phase 1 (Q-0039 (c)).
- **Local sign-in rate limits** (EZD-007d D1): `[auth.rate_limit]` `sign_in_sign_ups = 1000` and `token_verifications = 1000` in `supabase/config.toml`, local only, so one gate's many test sign-ins from 127.0.0.1 are not refused; cloud projects keep Supabase's limits.
- **Allowed Supabase client files** (EZD-007c T34): the only non-test files under `app/src/` that import `@supabase/*` as values are `app/src/server/auth/supabase-server.ts`, `app/src/server/db/client.ts` (`createPublicDbClient()`, anon, used only by `health.ts` for `rpc/health`), `app/src/server/jobs/db.ts` (`createJobsDbClient()`, secret key, job runner and the dev notifications route; EZD-009, EZD-323) and `app/src/lib/realtime/adapter.ts` (EZD-018, the browser Realtime client, no table-query API). A ticket that needs another client file edits T34's list in its own commit with a reason, and the security-reviewer approves it.

### Realtime (EZD-018)

EZD-018 (vault/plans/EZD-018.md Steps 1–8); 02 Stack Database; 03 rules 1–2; 11 rule 11; R26 R1; P4; Q-0052 (c); Q-0087 (ii); Q-0044 (b).

- **Topics.** `project:<project_id>` (lock state, presence, job progress, review count) and `user:<app_user_id>` (notifications, `session.ended`, EZD-102 permission changes), lower-case canonical UUIDs. Every channel is a Supabase Realtime **private** channel, so a join runs RLS on `realtime.messages`. Broadcast and Presence only; Postgres Changes are not used.
- **Who may join.** Two policies on `realtime.messages`, both `to authenticated` (none for `anon`): `realtime_messages_select` (receive broadcast and presence) through `private.realtime_can_receive(topic)`, and `realtime_messages_insert` (track presence only) through `private.realtime_can_track(topic)`. A `project:` topic needs `private.can_see_project(<id>)` (claim tenant and listed projects, read live, so a newly listed project is joinable on the next join, Q-0044 (b)); a `user:` topic needs `<id> = private.app_user_id()`; presence is project channels only; any other topic, a malformed id or an ended session is refused. `private.realtime_topic_id(topic, kind)` parses the id and never raises.
- **Clients never broadcast.** There is no insert policy for `broadcast`. Every event comes from the database through `private.broadcast_project(p_project_id, p_event, p_payload)` and `private.broadcast_user(p_app_user_id, p_event, p_payload)` (`security definer`, no API role may execute them; `22023` on a null argument or an event name not matching `^[a-z][a-z_]*\.[a-z][a-z_]*$`). Later tickets call them from triggers on the changed table, so an event is pushed only after its change commits: EZD-009 job progress, EZD-125 lock changes, EZD-127 review count on the project channel; EZD-110 notifications on the user channel. Each adds its event name to `app/src/lib/realtime/events.ts` (`ProjectChannelEvents`, `UserChannelEvents`) in the same commit as its trigger (T19 checks it).
- **Payload rule.** Every member who can see the project receives every project-channel message, and RLS checks project scope only, never permissions. A payload carries only ids, statuses, counters and timestamps: never money fields, model data, names or emails (03 rule 2). A subscriber that needs more refetches through a server route, where `can()` applies. `realtime.send` adds the message id as payload `id`; `toEventPayload` (`events.ts`) drops it.
- **`session.ended`.** `private.end_user_sessions(uuid)` (redefined in `plpgsql`, same signature and grants) also pushes `session.ended` `{}` on the user's channel whenever it ends their sessions (deactivation, removal, scope change, a narrowed project list; Q-0052 (c)). The hub then drops every channel, forgets the token and sends the person to `/sign-in`. The hard limit for a tampered browser is the access-token lifetime `jwt_expiry` = 3600 s.
- **Token route.** `GET /api/realtime/token` (`app/src/app/api/realtime/token/route.ts`) returns `{ url, apiKey, accessToken, expiresAt, appUserId }` (publishable key, the session's access token and its `expires_at` in seconds) with `Cache-Control: no-store`; 401 `error.not_signed_in` without a live session, 500 `error.db_unavailable` when the session read fails (Q-0168 (b)). The access token lives in browser memory only, for the Realtime socket; the refresh token stays in the `httpOnly` cookie. PostgREST refuses that token without the server-only gate header (Q-0087 (ii)), so it reads no table.
- **Hub.** `app/src/lib/realtime/` (`getRealtimeHub()` in `index.ts`, per tab, created on first use in the browser): `hub.project(projectId)` and `hub.user()` return handles (`status`, `ready`, `on(event, cb)`, `onResync(cb)`, `onStatus(cb)`, `release()`; project handles add `presence()` and `onPresence(cb)`); `react.ts` has `useProjectChannel` and `usePresence`. One channel per topic per tab, removed with the last handle; while any handle is open the hub also holds the user's own channel. Presence meta is `{ appUserId }` only (key = `appUserId`). The browser uses `@supabase/realtime-js` only, never `@supabase/supabase-js` (no table-query API in the browser). Entries are keyed by topic (`project:<id>`; the user channel apart), so no project id can share the user channel; a project id that is not a lower-case UUID after lower-casing gets a handle that turns `refused` on the next microtask, with no token fetch, no join and no hold on the user channel (EZD-157).
- **Requests only on events.** The hub fetches a token at first connect, once after a refused or closed channel, and once per socket reopen after a drop; it starts no interval and no repeating timeout. The library's heartbeat frames and socket reconnect backoff travel on the WebSocket, not as HTTP requests. A join refused twice is `refused` and never retried.
- **Resync.** Every `SUBSCRIBED` of a channel, the first one included, calls every `onResync` callback of every handle on it exactly once, so each subscriber refetches once per join: a change made between the server render and the first join, or while the channel was down or its token refused, is picked up (EZD-318; Q-0545, Q-0546). A handle taken on a channel that is already subscribed has its callbacks called once on the next microtask; a callback added to a handle after that handle's resync waits for the next `SUBSCRIBED`. After a drop (channel error, timeout, unexpected close, socket close) the status is `reconnecting`; on the next socket open, or when `useOnline`'s source reports online, the hub fetches a token once and rejoins. A refused token fetch sets `refused` on every channel not subscribed (a subscribed one is left as it is) and the next socket open or online report retries. `ready` always holds a channel's latest outcome: pending until the first, then a promise resolved `'subscribed'` or `'refused'`, replaced when the outcome changes.
- **No polling lint** (11 rule 11). An `eslint.config.mjs` block for `app/src/**/*.{ts,tsx}` (test files, `test-utils/` and `test-harness/` ignored) refuses `setInterval` (`no-restricted-globals`) and `window.`/`globalThis.`/`self.setInterval` (`no-restricted-properties`). A display-only timer uses `// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-globals -- display only, no network`, and the code-reviewer checks the reason. A repeating `setTimeout` loop is checked by review. A later block that also sets either rule for the same files must repeat these entries, because a later flat-config block replaces a rule's options.

### Password sign-in (EZD-033)

EZD-033 (vault/plans/EZD-033.md Steps 1-9); Q-0090; Q-0091; Q-0092; Q-0093; Q-0094 (override 2026-10-18: 8 characters); Q-0101; Q-0102; Q-0103; Q-0104; Q-0105.

- **Action.** `signInWithPassword(formData)` (`app/src/server/auth/actions.ts`, fields `email` and `password`): the email as `requestSignIn` reads it; the password as typed. Passwords are not normalized (no trimming, no Unicode normalization), so the same bytes work on every path, including direct Auth calls. Order: throttle slot, then an empty password or one over 72 UTF-8 bytes is `password_wrong` without calling Auth, then `auth.signInWithPassword` on the action's `createSessionClient()`. Success settles the slot, runs EZD-007's `completeSignIn` (link, access, invite acceptance with `user.joined`, refresh), clears the sign-in email cookie (`clearAppCookie`; `__Host-ezd_sign_in_email` on https) and redirects to `/`; `no_access` as for the code. An Auth 429 is `too_many_requests`; a network error or 5xx is `unavailable` (reported with the code `auth_password_unavailable`); **every other Auth error (wrong password, unknown email, unconfirmed account) is the one answer `password_wrong`** ("Email or password is wrong.", Q-0091). The password is never logged, put in an Error, returned or written to a cookie.
- **Page.** `/sign-in` step 1 (Q-0091 order): email, password (`autocomplete="current-password"`), Sign in (`intent=password`, the first submit, so Enter signs in), "Email me a sign-in link instead" (`intent=send`, `data-testid="sign-in-submit"`) and "Forgot password? Email me a code" (`intent=forgot`); both email buttons call `requestSignIn`, so "Forgot password?" sends the normal sign-in email with link and code. There is no reset flow and no reset form. `unavailable` shows `error.db_unavailable`.
- **Throttle** (Q-0094 limits, Q-0104 rules). Table `public.sign_in_throttle` (`id`, `kind` `ip`/`password`/`code`/`email`, `key_hash`, `failed`, `at`): tenant-less, RLS on with one deny-all policy, no privilege for `anon` or `authenticated`, keyed hashes and times only. Written only by `public.sign_in_throttle_take(p_kind, p_email_key, p_ip_key)` and `public.sign_in_throttle_settle(p_attempt_id, p_ip_attempt_id)` (owner-rights functions, empty search path, executable by `anon` and `authenticated` behind the request gate, in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`); `throttleTake` and `throttleSettle` in `app/src/server/auth/throttle.ts`. Limits: per IP 30 in 15 minutes; per email 5 wrong passwords in 15 minutes, 10 wrong codes in 15 minutes, 5 sign-in emails in 1 hour. No hard lockout, and the password and code counts are separate, so a password-throttled email can still sign in with the emailed code.
  - (i) What counts: toward the per-IP 30, every sign-in email request and every wrong password, wrong code or bad link; a success, an Auth 429, an Auth 5xx and an Auth captcha refusal (`captcha_failed`, Q-0251 (a)) settle the attempt's own rows (`failed = false`), which then no longer count toward the IP, password or code limits. Email requests are never settled.
  - (ii) A refused attempt inserts nothing and calls no Auth endpoint (`ip_throttled`, `password_throttled`, `code_throttled`, `email_throttled`, Q-0101).
  - (iii) A success settles only its own rows; it does not clear earlier failures.
  - (iv) Windows roll; rows older than 1 hour are deleted on each take; advisory locks (IP key, then email key) stop two requests from both taking the last slot.
  - (v) IPv4 counts by the full address, IPv6 by its /64 prefix, IPv4-mapped IPv6 as IPv4.
  - (vi) `clientIp(headers, env)` reads only `x-forwarded-for` (first entry), the client-address header Vercel sets and overwrites (https://vercel.com/docs/headers/request-headers, R-027; checked on the live site with `/dev/cloud-check`, docs/runbook.md `## Cloud checks (EZD-171)` J3). The test page `/dev/cloud-check` shows what `clientIp` reads, with and without a fake `x-forwarded-for`, and whether the session has the tenant claim and passes the API gate. Exception to the `/dev` rule (Q-0306 (a)): in production the page and its read route answer only to a request whose claims have `sub`, and give the same empty 404 as every other `/dev` page otherwise; the body is booleans and the caller's own parsed address, never a claim value or token. With `VERCEL_ENV` unset (local, CI) a loopback address or no header is `null` (no IP count) and an unparsable value is `unknown`; with `VERCEL_ENV` set it is never `null`: loopback, private, carrier-NAT, link-local, `fc00::/7`, `fe80::/10`, unparsable or missing all count as `unknown`.
  - (vii) Keys: `throttleKey(scope, value)` is the hex HMAC-SHA256 of `<scope>:<value>` under a key derived with HKDF-SHA256 from `SUPABASE_API_GATE_SECRET` (label `ezd-sign-in-throttle-v1`); the key is never sent to the database, stored or logged. No email or address reaches the database. Rotating the gate secret resets the counts.
  - Fails closed: a missing gate secret or a failed take reports `auth_throttle_unavailable` and returns `unavailable` without calling Auth.
- **Code and link actions.** `requestSignIn` takes an `email` slot after the email check; `verifySignInCode` a `code` slot and `confirmSignInLink` a `link` slot (IP only) after their input checks. A network error or 5xx from `verifyOtp` is now `unavailable` (reported), not `code_invalid` / `link_invalid`. Every report uses `{ source: 'function', location: 'auth.sign_in' }` and a code only.
- **Password rules** for screens that set a password: `validateNewPassword(password, email)` (`app/src/lib/auth/password-rules.ts`) returns `too_short` (fewer than `PASSWORD_MIN_CHARS` = 8 code points), `too_long` (more than `PASSWORD_MAX_BYTES` = 72 UTF-8 bytes), `contains_app_name` ("ezdraft" after lower-casing and removing spaces, `.`, `-`, `_`), `contains_email_name` (the email's local part, 4 or more code points, as a plain substring after lower-casing), in that order. No character-type rules; the breached-password check is Supabase's leaked-password protection, on in each cloud project (`docs/runbook.md`). Not used at sign-in.
- **Templates.** `supabase/templates/recovery.html` (Supabase's own reset email, sent only when someone calls the recovery endpoint directly; Q-0092 text, no link and no code) and `supabase/templates/reauthentication.html` (the confirmation code for a password change under `secure_password_change`, no link); English then Spanish; subjects in `supabase/config.toml` (Q-0102). Local `minimum_password_length = 8`, `password_requirements = ""`; leaked-password protection is off locally so no test reaches the breach service.
- **Guard kept.** EZD-007a's unconfirmed-password guard is unchanged: a password survives only on a confirmed account.
- **Sessions.** A password sign-in makes the same session as a code sign-in (400-day httpOnly cookies, ended on deactivation by EZD-006's triggers).
- **For EZD-105b and EZD-108** (setting passwords, Phase 1): call `validateNewPassword()` before setting; set passwords only on confirmed accounts (the guard blanks any other); EZD-105b ends the member's sessions; EZD-108 ends the user's other sessions and uses the reauthentication email.

Known limit (Q-0105). Accepted (Q-0105): direct Auth calls are limited only by Supabase's per-IP limits, the 8-character minimum, leaked-password protection and the guard; revisit per Q-0093 and EZD-1705.

### My profile (EZD-108)

EZD-108 (vault/plans/EZD-108.md Steps 1-18); S15 Avatar; S01 User avatar; Q-0094; Q-0096 P5, P6; Q-0392; Q-0412; Q-0447 (a); Q-0465 (a).

- **Functions** (`security definer`, empty search path, `authenticated` only, behind the request gate, in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`). `public.update_my_profile(p_language, p_ipad_input_mode)` writes only the caller's own `app_user` row (`language` `en`/`es`, `ipad_input_mode` `finger`/`pencil`; null or another value is `22023` `invalid_input`; no linked user `42501`); nothing changed means no update, so `updated_at` stays; returns `saved_language`, `saved_ipad_input_mode`, `language_changed`. `public.current_user_has_password()` is true when the caller's own `auth.users` row has a non-empty `encrypted_password` (a code-only user has none). `app_user` stays `select`-only.
- **Routes** (`requireApiSession`, every response `Cache-Control: no-store`, errors `{ error: { code, message_key } }`). `GET /api/profile` → `{ profile: { name, email, language, ipadDrawing, hasPassword }, captcha }` (a null iPad value reads as `finger`; `captcha` is `captchaConfig(process.env)`, read per request: `{ siteKey, scriptUrl }`, or null without `TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY`). `PATCH /api/profile` (`{ language, ipadDrawing }`) → `{ profile: { language, ipadDrawing } }`; a language change emits `user.language_changed` (`log_only`, `{ appUserId, language }`), and a dropped event does not fail the save but is reported (`profile_language_event_no_tenant`, `profile_language_event_failed`). `POST /api/profile/password` (`{ mode: 'change', currentPassword, newPassword, confirmPassword, captchaToken? }` or `{ mode: 'set', newPassword, confirmPassword, code? }`) → `{ status: 'done' | 'code_sent' }`. Codes: `invalid_input`, `captcha_failed`, `password_too_short`, `password_too_long`, `password_contains_app_name`, `password_contains_email_name`, `password_mismatch`, `current_password_wrong`, `password_same`, `password_breached`, `code_invalid` (400); `current_password_throttled`, `code_throttled`, `ip_throttled`, `too_many_requests` (429); `not_signed_in` (401); `db_unavailable` (500).
- **Password flow** (`app/src/server/profile/password.ts`). Both modes check `validateNewPassword` and the match first. Change: a `password` throttle slot (Q-0094: 5 wrong per 15 minutes per email); the current password is checked by `signInWithPassword` on a fresh in-memory session (`createMemoryJar()`) with the security-check token the dialog sends (Q-0465 (a); Auth checks the captcha on password sign-in only, so Set has no widget), and a wrong one is not settled, so it counts (Auth's `captcha_failed` → `captcha_failed`, settled, not reported); the new password is set on that fresh session (`same_password` → `password_same`, `weak_password` → `password_breached`); Auth then ends every other session, the cookie session too, and `setSession` moves this device onto the fresh session (a failure is reported `profile_session_move_failed` and the fresh session signs out locally; the answer stays 200). Set (no password yet): on the cookie session; a session older than 24 h gets `reauthentication_needed`, so `reauthenticate()` emails Auth's code (the reauthentication template) and the answer is `code_sent`; the next submit sends the code as `nonce` behind a `code` throttle slot; a code that is not six digits (`codeProblem`) is `code_invalid` before any slot or Auth call (`reauthentication_not_valid`, or `reauthentication_needed` with a code, → `code_invalid`). Each success sends one English system email through `sendEmail` (`office.profile.email_changed_*` / `email_set_*`, link to the app home, no `company`, no Copy link); a failed send does not fail the route. Passwords and codes are never logged, put in an Error, returned or stored.
- **UI.** `AvatarMenu` (`app/src/components/account/`, built on `Menu`, see Menus (EZD-108); anchored on the header card) replaces the Settings header initials (`data-settings-avatar` stays inside the trigger): My profile, Company settings (with at least one Settings section allowed), Sign out. In Settings, Company settings goes through `useLeave` and Sign out through `useLeaveCheck` (`unsaved-changes.tsx`), so unsaved edits ask first. `MyProfileDialog` uses Radix `Dialog` and `Tabs` (`PROFILE_TABS = ['profile']`; EZD-140 adds Notifications); in Change password it mounts `Captcha` under Confirm when `captcha` is not null, and the button waits for a token.
- **Icons** added: `logout`, `person`, `settings`.

### AI assistant keys (EZD-5601)

EZD-5601 (vault/plans/EZD-5601.md section 1); S01g; Q-0637 key storage and call path; Q-0638 provider errors and rotation.

- **Tables.** `public.user_ai_key` (`tenant_id`, `app_user_id`, `ciphertext` (base64, 40-512 characters), `secret_version` (1-999999), `last4` (4 printable ASCII characters), timestamps; primary key tenant and user) and `public.ai_key_attempt` (`id`, `tenant_id`, `app_user_id`, `at`). RLS on with one deny-all policy each; no `anon` or `authenticated` privilege (`TABLE_GRANTS` `privileges: []`); `service_role` all four.
- **Functions** (`security definer`, empty search path, `authenticated` only, behind the request gate, in `GATED_RPC_CALLS`; user and tenant from the claims). `get_my_ai_key()` always answers one row (key columns null without a key or a tenant claim); `save_my_ai_key(tenant, user, ciphertext, version, last4)` (`42501` unless the ids are the caller's own with an active membership; `22023` for a value outside the column checks); `delete_my_ai_key()` returns `(deleted, last4)`; `take_ai_key_attempt()` (5 per user per rolling 15 minutes; the caller's `app_user` row is locked before the count); `rewrap_my_ai_key(old_version, ciphertext, version)` (writes only while the row still has `old_version`). Trigger `membership_drop_ai_key` (`private.membership_drop_ai_key()`) deletes the key row when the membership is deleted, stops being `active`, or changes person or company.
- **Cipher** (`app/src/server/assistant/key-crypto.ts`). AES-256-GCM, 12-byte random IV, 16-byte tag, AAD `ezd-ai-key-v1:<tenantId>:<appUserId>`; `ciphertext` is base64 of IV, encrypted bytes and tag. `AI_KEY_ENCRYPTION_KEYS` is read per call: comma-separated `n:<base64 of 32 bytes>` (n 1-999999, no repeats); unset or blank is `ai_key_config_missing`, any bad entry `ai_key_config_invalid`. New keys use the highest number; a row decrypts with its own `secret_version`.
- **Key check** (`checkAnthropicKey`, `app/src/server/ai/key-check.ts`): `GET /v1/models` with the user's key (`UserAiKey`, which prints `[redacted]`), a 20,000 ms budget and the SDK `logLevel` off; stub recording `anthropic/key-check`. `classifyAnthropicError`: 401 and 403 key refused; 402, a 400 usage-limit message or a 429 `enforced_spend_limit_reached` out of credit; other 429 rate limited; other 4xx request refused; 5xx or no status unavailable.
- **Routes** (`requireApiSession`, every response `Cache-Control: no-store`). `POST /api/profile/ai-key` (`{ key }`, body at most 2,048 characters) answers 200 `{ aiKey: { last4 } }`; order: 401, body (`invalid_input`), shape (`ai_key_invalid`), encryption setting (500 `ai_key_unavailable`, reported), own row (403 `not_authorized`), attempt slot (429 `ai_key_throttled`), key check (400 `ai_key_refused`, 400 `ai_key_out_of_credit`, 502 `ai_key_check_failed`; an `AiError` is 500 `ai_key_unavailable`, reported), then encrypt and save. `DELETE /api/profile/ai-key` answers 200 `{ aiKey: null }`. `GET /api/profile` adds `aiKey: { last4 } | null`. Every signed-in user may (Q-0638).
- **Use and rotation.** `loadUserAiKeyForCall(client)` (for EZD-5602) answers `none`, `key` or `undecryptable` (reported `ai_key_undecryptable`, row untouched) and rewraps a row below the highest version on use (a failure is reported `ai_key_rewrap_failed`). The sweep runs `rewrapStaleAiKeys()` (`app/src/server/jobs/ai-key-rewrap.ts`) after `runJobs`: up to 200 rows per sweep with the runner's client; a failure is reported `ai_key_rewrap_failed` and the sweep still answers.
- **Emails.** One English system email per add and per remove (`office.profile.email_ai_key_added_*`, `email_ai_key_removed_*`, with the last 4 characters and a link to the app home; no `company`, no Copy link); a failed send does not change the answer; no email when a membership ends. The key is never logged, returned, reported or put in an Error.

### Menus (EZD-108)

EZD-108 (vault/plans/EZD-108.md Step 12a, Decided "Menu primitive"); 11 Budgets; WAI-ARIA APG Menu Button (https://www.w3.org/WAI/ARIA/apg/patterns/menu-button/).

- `Menu`, `MenuItem` and `MenuSeparator` from `app/src/components/menu/` are the menu for the office, field-pack and client-link entries; never Radix `DropdownMenu` there (the Radix menu, `@floating-ui` and collection cost 19,669 compressed bytes of the 200 KB budget).
- Trigger: a `button` with `aria-haspopup="menu"`, `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls` while open, `data-state` `open`/`closed`, `aria-label` and `title`. Closed, the menu is not rendered; open, it is portalled into `document.body`, `role="menu"`, fixed `sideOffset` (default 8) below the anchor with right edges lined up; the anchor is `trigger.closest(anchorSelector)` when `anchorSelector` is given and matches, else the trigger; a window resize closes it.
- Keys: click, Enter, Space or ArrowDown open on the first item, ArrowUp on the last; in the menu ArrowDown / ArrowUp with wrap, Home, End; Escape closes and focuses the trigger; Tab closes, focuses the trigger and keeps the browser default. Choosing an item closes the menu, focuses the trigger, then runs `onSelect`. A press or focus outside closes it. No typeahead, no submenus, non-modal.

### Captcha (EZD-034)

EZD-034 (vault/plans/EZD-034.md Steps 1-13); 11 Security and audit; Q-0175 (b); Q-0241; Q-0242 (a); Q-0243 (c); Q-0251 (a); Q-0252; Q-0153.

- **Rules** (Q-0243 (c)): Supabase Auth's captcha (Cloudflare Turnstile) stays off locally and in every test (`[auth.captcha]` in `supabase/config.toml` stays commented out); no test loads Cloudflare's script or calls Cloudflare (CLAUDE.md rule 6). With Auth's captcha off, Auth ignores any token. The app never verifies a token itself; Auth does.
- **Site key.** `captchaConfig(env)` (`app/src/server/auth/captcha-config.ts`) reads `TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY` per request and trims it; empty or unset is `null`: no widget, no `captcha_token` field, step-1 buttons enabled as before. Otherwise `{ siteKey, scriptUrl: TURNSTILE_SCRIPT_URL }`, passed to `SignInForm` as a prop (not `NEXT_PUBLIC_`, which is inlined at build).
- **Widget and token field.** `Captcha` (`app/src/components/auth/Captcha.tsx`) loads the provider's script itself (explicit render, not `next/script`), renders the widget below the password box in the device's sign-in language and hands the token to `SignInForm`, which sends it as the hidden step-1 field `captcha_token` and keeps the three step-1 buttons disabled until a token exists. Tokens are single-use, so the widget resets after every step-1 answer. A failed script load shows `error.captcha_unavailable`. Step 2 (the code) and `/auth/confirm` have no check.
- **Actions.** `requestSignIn` and `signInWithPassword` pass `captcha_token` to Auth as `captchaToken`. An Auth refusal is matched on the error code `captcha_failed` only, never on HTTP 400 alone, and is answered `captcha_failed` (`error.captcha_failed`), not reported to Sentry. On a password sign-in it settles the slot (Q-0251 (a)); on a code request it still counts and sets no `ezd_sign_in_email` cookie (Q-0252).
- **Test page.** `/dev/captcha` (not found in production) shows the step-1 form with Cloudflare's always-pass test site key `TURNSTILE_TEST_SITE_KEY` and the same-origin stub script `GET /dev/captcha/stub` (`DEV_CAPTCHA_STUB_URL`, 404 in production), which sets `window.turnstile` and `window.__captchaStub`; tokens are issued only by `__captchaStub.issue()`.
- **Cloudflare's host** `challenges.cloudflare.com` is named in `app/src` only in `captcha-config.ts`.
- **Hand check.** `scripts/auth/captcha-check.mjs`, run by Josh by hand (`docs/runbook.md` `## Captcha (EZD-034)` step 5), refused under `CI`, never in a root script or the gate: it asks Auth for a sign-in code without a token and passes only on HTTP 400 `captcha_failed`.

## Migrations

02 Named tools; 11 Operations; vault/tickets/README.md; EZD-023a.

- Folder `supabase/migrations/`. File name `<YYYYMMDDHHMMSS>_<snake_case_description>.sql`: a 14-digit UTC timestamp as `supabase migration new` writes it, then a description in lowercase letters, digits and underscores, e.g. `20261012093000_create_tenant.sql`. Timestamps are unique (check C5).
- One concern per file.
- Add before remove: never drop or rename what the previous release reads (11 Operations; check by EZD-023a, `### Release safety (EZD-023a)`).
- A migration that creates a table also adds `tenant_id`, its RLS policy, the `tenant_id` and `project_id` indexes and the tenant-isolation test (tickets README; 11 rule 10; EZD-006).
- TypeScript types are generated from the local database by the Supabase CLI into `app/src/server/db/database.types.ts` and committed (02 Named tools; EZD-004a adds the script).
- A function that counts rows for a cap locks (D4 forms) before its first count(…); no policy counts. Read-only counters: app/test-harness/rls/cap-locks.ts (EZD-321).

### Local database

EZD-004a (vault/plans/EZD-004a.md Steps 2–6); 02 Named tools; 11 Operations "local (agents and tests)". The CLI is the root `supabase` devDependency, run through the root `db:*` scripts.

- `pnpm db:up`: `supabase status`, `supabase start` if the stack is not running, or `supabase stop` then `supabase start` when the SHA-256 of `supabase/config.toml` and `supabase/templates/*` differs from the marker `supabase/.temp/ezd-stack-config-sha256` of the main checkout (shared by every worktree, since they share one stack; written after each successful start; EZD-166; lane n: `ezd-stack-config-sha256-lane<n>` in the same folder, over the hash of `.lanes/lane<n>/supabase/config.toml` and `.lanes/lane<n>/supabase/templates/*`, EZD-172), then `supabase db reset --local` every time, so every run applies every migration to a fresh database and wipes local data. After storing the gate secret it restarts the PostgREST container `supabase_rest_<project_id>` (lane n: `supabase_rest_ezdraft-lane<n>`, EZD-172) and waits until a request without the gate header gets `api_gate` (`scripts/db/api-gate-ready.mjs`; EZD-190, Q-0330), because `db reset` restarts Postgres but not PostgREST, whose reconnect can reload its config without `pgrst.db_pre_request`; a bare `pnpm db:reset` can leave PostgREST without the gate, so use `pnpm db:up`. It then joins one public Realtime channel and waits until the slot `supabase_realtime_messages_replication_slot_` is active (`scripts/db/realtime-warmup.mjs`), because `db reset` restarts Realtime and Realtime starts that stream only at the first join after a start, so the first database broadcast after a reset would be lost (EZD-035). The root `test` script runs it first, so every gate run (and CI) starts from a reset stack. Without Docker it exits 1 with `Local Supabase needs Docker running (start Docker Desktop).` on stderr. Kong must serve this checkout's email templates (EZD-287).
- `pnpm db:env`: prints one JSON object with four keys: `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` and `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY` from `supabase status` (the CLI's published local defaults, not secrets), and `SUPABASE_API_GATE_SECRET`, the trimmed content of `supabase/.temp/ezd-api-gate-secret` (EZD-007c; lane n: `.lanes/lane<n>/supabase/.temp/ezd-api-gate-secret`, EZD-172); exit 1 when the stack is down, and exit 1 with no output and `supabase/.temp/ezd-api-gate-secret is missing: run pnpm db:up` (lane n: `.lanes/lane<n>/supabase/.temp/ezd-api-gate-secret is missing: run pnpm db:up`, EZD-172) on stderr when that file is missing or blank. `pnpm db:reset` and `pnpm db:stop` first run `node scripts/db/local.mjs prepare`, then call the CLI (`supabase db reset --local`, `supabase stop`) on the current lane's stack (EZD-172).
- `pnpm supabase migration new <name>` writes the next migration file. After every migration run `pnpm db:types` and commit the result; `node scripts/db/gen-types.mjs --check` (test T9) fails the gate when the committed file differs from the local database.
- `gen-types.mjs` (`pnpm db:types` and `--check`) holds one lock, `ezd-gen-types.lock` in the shared `supabase/.temp` of the main checkout (EZD-200), so concurrent checks take turns on the one stack. The lock is a directory taken by renaming a folder that already holds its owner file into place; a dead owner's lock is taken over by unlinking that owner's own file name, an empty lock dir is replaced at once, and a lock whose owner cannot be identified is never removed (it fails after 120 s with `gen_types_lock_timeout ... pid unknown`). A failed check names its cause on the first line of stderr: `gen_types_cli_failed` (the command, exit, signal and first stderr line), `gen_types_mismatch` (first differing line and the database and checkout migration versions) or `gen_types_lock_timeout`.
- `supabase/config.toml`: `project_id = "ezdraft"` (lane 0: one local stack shared by every worktree, one gate at a time, Q-0114; lanes 1-3: project `ezdraft-lane<n>` in `.lanes/lane<n>/supabase/config.toml`, one gate per lane, see Build lanes, EZD-172), `[analytics] enabled = false`, `[edge_runtime] enabled = false` (jobs are Vercel Functions, P4); everything else at the CLI default. `supabase/seed.sql` holds tenant 1 and Josh (EZD-005b) and tenant 2 with its owner and 200 tenant 1 projects (EZD-016c); see the Tenancy tables (EZD-005) subsection.
- The first migration creates `public.health()`: `security definer`, `search_path = ''`, execute granted to `anon` and `authenticated` only; it returns one row `('ok', <newest applied migration version>)`, read through `POST /rest/v1/rpc/health`. It is a function, not a table, so EZD-006's table checks do not cover it.
- `GET /api/health` answers the 500 `db_unavailable` body when the `health` RPC has not answered within `HEALTH_DB_TIMEOUT_MS` (`app/src/server/db/health.ts`, 3,000 ms, Q-0285 (a)), so a hung database never holds the request; the value stays below the release check's 4,000 ms per-request abort (`HEALTH_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`, EZD-023e).

Cloud dev: `.github/workflows/supabase-dev.yml` applies `supabase/migrations/` to the cloud project `ezdraft-dev` through `node scripts/db/dev-push.mjs` (EZD-146: the release's `createSupabaseCli().push` with `MIGRATION_TIMEOUTS`; `--db-url` without the password, the password only in `PGPASSWORD`) on each develop push that changes them and by Run workflow (Q-0107); without the `SUPABASE_DEV_DB_URL` secret it ends green and pushes nothing. Agents never run `db push` or `link` themselves (`.claude/hooks/guard-bash.mjs`). Josh's setup: `docs/runbook.md`, Supabase cloud projects.

On Josh's Mac a local gate's stack runs from the run copy `/Users/ezdraft/gate/lane<n>/tree`, as the ezdraft user (EZD-354, Gate user); builders' own `pnpm db:up` still runs from their worktree.

### Build lanes (EZD-172)

EZD-172 (vault/plans/EZD-172.md Steps 1-9); Josh 2026-10-24 (boost).

- **Lanes.** `EZD_LANE` is `0` (unset or empty: today's single stack) or `1`-`3`; anything else is `lane_invalid`. Lane n has its own Supabase project folder `<repo>/.lanes/lane<n>/` (git-ignored), which the CLI reaches through `SUPABASE_WORKDIR`, so up to three builds run their checks at once without wiping each other's data. Code: `scripts/db/lane.mjs`.
- **Values.** Lane 0 / 1 / 2 / 3: project `ezdraft` / `-lane1` / `-lane2` / `-lane3`; api 54321 / 54421 / 54521 / 54621; db 54322 / 54422 / 54522 / 54622; shadow 54320 / 54420 / 54520 / 54620; pooler 54329 / 54429 / 54529 / 54629; studio 54323 / 54423 / 54523 / 54623; mail 54324 / 54424 / 54524 / 54624; analytics 54327 / 54427 / 54527 / 54627; inspector 8083 / 8183 / 8283 / 8383; web 3000 / 3010 / 3020 / 3030; perf 3200 / 3210 / 3220 / 3230. The scratch restore stack of lane n uses 25320-25329 + 100 n and project `ezdraft-restore-lane<n>`.
- **Folder.** `node scripts/db/local.mjs prepare` (also run first by `up`, `db:reset` and `db:stop`) syncs `.lanes/lane<n>/supabase/` from the committed `supabase/`: `config.toml` with the lane's project id, ports and auth URLs, `seed.sql`, `migrations/`, `templates/`; `.temp/` is never touched. A failed sync in `up` prints one line and exits 1 with no CLI call. `node scripts/db/local.mjs lane` prints the lane's values and the two env names as one JSON line.
- **Restart marker.** lane n: ezd-stack-config-sha256-lane<n> in the same folder, over the lane folder's config and templates; EZD-172.
- **Env check.** `up`, `env`, `prepare` and `stop-lane` refuse (exit 1) when `SUPABASE_WORKDIR` does not match `EZD_LANE`. The gate sets both for lane n and clears `SUPABASE_WORKDIR` for lane 0.
- **Gate lock.** One gate per lane: `ezdraft-gate.lock` (lane 0, Q-0019) or `ezdraft-gate-lane<n>.lock` in the temp folder. Lane stacks 1-3 stop at the end of every gate (`node scripts/db/local.mjs stop-lane`, Q-0354 (a)), because Supabase CLI 2.121.0 publishes every stack port on all addresses and has no setting to bind them to 127.0.0.1; lane 0's stack keeps running; `pnpm db:stop` stops the current lane's stack. A gate or sweep lock holds its owner's pid from the moment it appears: the pid is written to `<lock>.<pid>.tmp` and that file is hard-linked to the lock name (Q-0381). A gate that finds an empty gate lock (an older gate script opens the file before it writes the pid) reads it again every 50 ms for up to 2,000 ms before it treats the lock as dead. Only one gate at a time removes a dead gate or sweep lock (EZD-256): it first takes the lock's take-over folder `<lock>.takeover` by renaming a temp folder `<lock>.takeover.<pid>.tmp` that holds one file `<pid>-<uuid>` onto that name, which works only while the folder is missing or empty; it then reads the lock again, removes it only when it still holds the text it judged dead, and frees the folder. A take-over folder whose file names a dead process is emptied by the next gate; while a live gate holds it, a gate refuses as it does for a held lock and a sweep skips that lane. A file holding the reader's own pid counts as dead. A holder file whose pid now belongs to another process counts as live until that process ends; gates refuse meanwhile and nothing is removed. Code: `scripts/gate-lock.mjs`.
- **Stale lane stacks.** A lane 1-3 gate, after its install step, stops the stack of each other lane 1-3 that no live gate holds (`ezdraft-gate-lane<m>.lock`) and no live lane lease holds (`ezdraft-lane<m>.lease` in the temp folder, holding the pid of the process using the lane; the launcher writes it, EZD-174), so a stack left by a killed gate or a crash (Docker restarts it, restart policy `unless-stopped`) closes at the next lane gate (Q-0367 (b)). Lane 0 gates never do this. Run a lane by hand outside a gate only with that lease file holding your shell's pid.

A local lane gate's stack runs from `/Users/ezdraft/gate/lane<n>/tree` (EZD-354), so its `SUPABASE_WORKDIR` is `<that tree>/.lanes/lane<n>`; the lane lock and lease rules are unchanged.

### Tenancy tables (EZD-005)

EZD-005a (vault/plans/EZD-005a.md Steps 1, 5); 03 Tables tenant, user, membership, project; 03 intro; 11 rule 10; 11 Security and audit. Six migrations after the health migration: `create_set_updated_at`, `create_tenant`, `create_app_user`, `create_project`, `create_membership`, `create_membership_project`. RLS is enabled on every table with no policy; EZD-006 adds the policies, the token hook and the grants.

- The Data model `user` entity is table `public.app_user`: `user` is a reserved word in PostgreSQL.
- Tenant-less tables, the list EZD-006's CI test starts from (every later exemption needs its own reason here): `tenant` (it is the tenant; key `id`), `app_user` (one person across tenants, linked to each company by `membership (user_id, tenant_id)`), `sign_in_throttle` (EZD-033: rows are written before sign-in and belong to no company; they hold only keyed hashes and times, Q-0094), `backup_run` (EZD-025: system-wide off-site backup and restore-drill records; one run covers every tenant; no tenant data; no API role can read it) and `error_tunnel_throttle` (EZD-143: the per-address count of error-tunnel events; rows are written for anonymous error reports and belong to no company; keyed hashes and times only; no API role can read it).
- A tenant-owned row that points at another tenant-owned row uses a composite foreign key carrying `tenant_id`: `membership` and `project` have `unique (id, tenant_id)`, and `membership_project` references `(membership_id, tenant_id)` and `(project_id, tenant_id)`, both `on delete cascade`, so a listed project can never belong to another company.
- Enumerated values are `text` columns with a `check` constraint, lower snake case (widening is add before remove, 11 Operations). `updated_at` on `tenant`, `app_user`, `project` and `membership` is kept by the `before update` trigger function `public.set_updated_at()` (`security invoker`, `search_path = ''`); `membership_project` has no `updated_at`.
- `tenant.contact` is JSON with keys `phone`, `email`, `website`, `address` (S15a Contact); `contact.email` is the company email replies go to (Q-0036; EZD-008). There is no separate reply-email column.
- `app_user.language` defaults to `en`; the inviter or the user switches it to `es` (Q-0029 (a); Q-0121 (a)).
- `app_user.auth_user_id` (unique, `references auth.users(id) on delete set null`) is filled at first sign-in by EZD-007.
- Indexes: `project_tenant_id_idx`, `membership_user_id_idx` (the EZD-006 token hook), `membership_project_tenant_id_idx`, `membership_project_project_id_idx`; `membership unique (tenant_id, user_id)` leads with `tenant_id` (11 rule 10).
- Seed rows (EZD-005b, vault/plans/EZD-005b.md Steps 1–3; Q-0033 (a); Q-0106; Q-0113): fixed ids tenant 1 = `00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001` (Worth Properties) and Josh = `00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000001001` (owner, membership `active` / `all`); `app/test-utils/seed-ids.ts` exports them as `TENANT_1_ID` and `JOSH_USER_ID`. `supabase/seed.sql` is local only (`supabase db reset --local`). Cloud projects get the same rows from `supabase/bootstrap/tenant-1.sql`, run once: dev by Josh through the SQL Editor (Q-0033 (a); `docs/runbook.md` Tenant 1 setup), prod by EZD-023b's release workflow on the first real release (Q-0106, Q-0113). The two files hold the same ids and values except the email (`josh@example.com` in the seed; typed in place of `PASTE-YOUR-EMAIL-HERE` in the bootstrap file); the placeholder (exactly once) and the codes `ezd_bootstrap_email_missing`, `ezd_bootstrap_already_done`, `ezd_bootstrap_done` are a contract with EZD-023b, and the file is one all-or-nothing `do $$ … $$;` block. `scripts/db/sql.mjs` `runLocalSql(sqlText)` is the local-only SQL runner (`docker exec … psql` into `supabase_db_<project_id>`, no connection argument).
- Local test rows (EZD-016c, vault/plans/EZD-016c.md Step 1; Q-0033 (a); Q-0198): `supabase/seed.sql` also holds tenant 2 (`Test Tenant 2`, id `00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002`), its owner (`tenant2-owner@example.com`, id `00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000002001`, membership `active` / `all`) and 200 tenant 1 projects (`Seed project 001` … `200`, ids from `00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000100001`). They are never in `supabase/bootstrap/tenant-1.sql`: the "same ids and values" rule between the two files covers tenant 1 and Josh only. `app/test-utils/seed-ids.ts` exports `TENANT_2_ID`, `TENANT_2_OWNER_USER_ID`, `JOSH_EMAIL`, `TENANT_2_OWNER_EMAIL`, `SEED_PROJECT_COUNT` and `seedProjectId(i)`. Seed rule: tests never change seeded rows; a test that changes data creates its own rows with the EZD-006 or EZD-007a fixtures and removes them.

### Row-level security (EZD-006)
